| 35 | src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/policy-center/index.ts:282 — break-glass `requiresApproval=subjectType!=="system"`, so `system` principals bypass break-glass approval | `done` | Root cause: the break-glass logic mistakenly treated system principals as inherently trusted, missing the unified approval gate. |

| 38 | src/platform/five-plane-control-plane/config-center/config-hot-reload-service.ts:451-457 — 32-bit non-cryptographic hash for config checksum; collision probability is noticeable | `done` | Root cause: hot-reload version numbers used a lightweight string hash rather than a strong integrity digest. |

| 78 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/distributed-lock/sqlite-lock-adapter.ts:140` — `forceSteal` hardcodes `ttlMs=30000` instead of reusing the original lock config | `done` | Root cause: the force-steal path evolved from an example implementation and hardcoded the TTL constant in the branch, not reusing the lock-config policy. |
| 79 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/distributed-lock/sqlite-lock-adapter.ts:107-112` — on `release`, owner mismatch silently returns `false` with no audit event | `done` | Root cause: release failure was long modeled as a boolean result, without treating owner mismatch as an auditable contention event. |

| 82 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/queue/redis-queue-adapter.ts:566-568` — if state is not `waiting`, `zrem` returns null and the job is silently dropped | `done` | Root cause: the old `dequeue` merged contention loss and abnormal task state into the same path, with no explicit recovery / requeue branch. |
| 83 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/queue/redis-queue-adapter.ts:592-596` — only the `ack` path expires the task key; nack/failed tasks accumulate unbounded | `done` | Root cause: the task retention policy only covered the success path; no unified TTL reclaim was defined for nack, dead-letter, and failure states. |
| 84 | `src/platform/five-plane-execution/queue/redis-queue-adapter.ts:605-609` — nack requeue has no backoff; the same worker immediately re-picks it | `done` | Root cause: the nack implementation only pursued "retry as soon as possible", without a minimum backoff window to avoid hot loops. |

| 192 | `migrate-sqlite-to-pg.ts` — column/table names directly concatenated into SQL, no whitelist (injection risk) | `done` | Root cause: the migration script previously assumed table and column names came from a trusted schema; the current implementation allowlists tables and validates identifiers before concatenation. |
| 193 | `idempotency-key-storage.ts` — `${this.tableName}` directly concatenated into SQL, not validated at construction | `done` | Root cause: idempotency-key storage once allowed unconstrained custom table names; it now forces validation of safe SQL identifiers at the construction boundary and fails close with a structured error. |

| 195 | `checkpoint-gc-service.ts` — `fs.stat` → `fs.unlink` TOCTOU window | `done` | Root cause: the GC delete path did an existence check before deleting; it has been changed to directly bind object identity with `lstat/open(O_NOFOLLOW)/fstat/unlink`, and returns idempotently on `ENOENT`. |
| 196 | `shadow-snapshot-service.ts` — symlink swap window between `lstat` and `rename` | `done` | Root cause: shadow-snapshot metadata previously overwrote the target via temporary-file `rename()`, separating `lstat` from the final landing point; it now uses `O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW` to exclusively create the final file, rejecting duplicate `snapshotId`. |

| 256 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:62-194` — 5 `Map`s + 2 LRUs in memory, `storageDir` mostly `null` from callers; the LRU's "intentionally not updating position on remove" violates semantics | `done` | Root cause: the connector framework previously kept manifest/instance/breaker registration state resident in memory with no overall capacity cap, and the bindings/health LRUs only refreshed on writes, so read accesses did not update the LRU order; capacity control with `maxConnectors` has been added for connector registration, and access-refresh / load-trim for bindings/health have been filled in. |
| 257 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:144-156` — the eviction loop contains "no progress → break" placeholder logic that masks real bugs | `done` | Root cause: the bindings-eviction logic previously relied on a "no progress → break" placeholder branch to prevent infinite loops; `evictLRUBindings()` now returns the actual number of removals, and if the removal count is 0, it explicitly throws an invariant error instead of silently swallowing the eviction exception. |
| 258 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:289-332` — the `failed` state short-circuits before the circuit breaker; the breaker does not increment; `success===false` is converted to `throw`, causing the breaker to double-count | `done` | Root cause: the execution path previously short-circuited `health-failed` outside the breaker and disguised `success=false` results as exceptions thrown back to the breaker; `health-failed` now passes through the breaker to count as one failure, and logical failure results are no longer artificially re-thrown. |
| 259 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:392-414` — provider name normalization for `servicenow`/`service-now`/`service_now` and GitHub case-inconsistent | `done` | Root cause: the built-in connector assembly logic previously scattered provider string variants across `switch` cases; it is now closed into `normalizeConnectorProvider()`, uniformly canonicalizing case, whitespace, underscores, and the `service-now` variant. |
| 260 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:494-509` — every `register`/`bind`/`recordHealth` fully serializes 3 `Map`s to disk, no batching / debounce | `done` | Root cause: any manifest/binding/health change in the connector framework previously rewrote all three collections to disk; manifest, binding, and health are now persisted separately by change domain, eliminating the per-write 3× full-JSON amplification. |
| 261 | `integration/connector-framework-service.ts:115-121` — repeated `register` of the same `connectorId` silently overwrites with no event | `done` | Root cause: the registration logic previously did `Map#set` overwrite for duplicate `connectorId`; it now explicitly throws `connector_framework.duplicate_connector_id`. |
| 262 | `tests/unit/scale-ecosystem/integration/connector-framework-service.test.ts:513` — `/tmp/connector-framework-test-${Date.now()}` is not cleaned up | `done` | Root cause: the unit test previously hand-wrote `/tmp` paths and did its own directory cleanup; it is now switched to the unified `createTempWorkspace()/cleanupPath()` helper. |

| 266 | `agent-delegation/delegation-manager.service.ts:847`, `delegation-manager-support.ts:104`, `hitl-operator-console-service.ts:63` — `Promise.all(...)`; a single failure aborts the whole batch, no per-item fallback | `done` | Root cause: repository fan-out and HITL notification fan-out previously assumed all backends were reliably available; they now use `Promise.allSettled()`, so a single status query or notification failure does not bring down the whole batch. |
| 267 | `harness/loop/index.ts:91`, `harness/recovery-controller.ts:39` — backoff jitter uses `Math.random()`, breaking reproducibility | `done` | Root cause: harness backoff used random jitter rather than deterministic jitter derived from the run context; it is now deterministic jitter derived from run/attempt, making replay and tests reproducible. |
| 268 | `harness/hitl-runtime.ts:71,465` — 30-day TTL double literal, no single source of truth | `done` | Root cause: HITL request and responsibility records each hand-wrote the 30-day expiration value; they now share `requestTtlMs` and `computeExpiryIso()`. |
| 269 | `harness/memory-manager.ts:34,168` — `shared:1000` and `30*60*1000` LRU window hardcoded, no config | `done` | Root cause: harness memory-tier capacity and demotion idle window were long hardcoded as module constants; `HarnessMemoryManagerOptions` is now exposed, allowing overrides of tier caps and the demotion idle window. |
| 270 | `harness-decision-manager.ts:186` — uses a comment in place of an interface constraint | `done` | Root cause: decision-evidence persistence previously received the canonical input bundle but suppressed unused fields with a comment; the bundle's key structure is now written into evidence `content`/`metadata`, no longer pretending to bind the interface through comments. |
| 271 | `contracts/execution-receipt/index.ts:64-67`, `harness-decision-manager.ts:185`, `quorum-calculator.ts:249`, `sub-workflow-executor.ts:731`, `assessment-service.ts:141`, `pack-routes.ts:107,121,143,182`, `incident-routes.ts:150`, `risk-evaluation-port.ts:26`, `inter-plane-contract-gateway.ts:332` — many places `void param;` discarding a parameter the signature claims to depend on; tenant / principal authorization is effectively bypassed | `done` | Root cause: many compatibility entry points evolved from "receive context then decide whether to consume it" into directly `void`-ing the input; most dangerously, the pack/incident APIs already authorize but do not push the tenant boundary all the way down to the service layer; pack routes are now closed off as global tenant-prohibited, incident service and routes now explicitly filter/update by tenant, and legacy receipt, quorum, and risk evaluation now at least validate input consistency, no longer accepting parameters that are nominally important but actually discarded. |

| 350 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/apps/web/runtime.test.tsx` — uses `mockReturnValueOnce` chain; a prior `expect` failure lets the queued mock leak into the next test | `done` | Root cause: when suite cleanup was insufficient, the `mockReturnValueOnce` queue could cross tests; mocks are now cleared in the related suite's `afterEach` to avoid queued leftovers. |
| 351 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/packages/features/approval/web.test.tsx:43` — uses real `Date.now()` instead of `vi.useFakeTimers`; snapshot is unstable across runs | `done` | Root cause: the approval view test directly depended on the real clock; it is now switched to fake timers and a fixed system time. |
| 352 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/packages/features/approval/web.test.tsx:72-74` — uses `fireEvent.click` instead of `userEvent.click`, missing pointer-down / keyboard semantics | `done` | Root cause: the approval interaction test only triggered `click`; it now also includes a `pointerDown + click` sequence for more realistic event semantics. |
| 353 | `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts:14` — CSP `script-src 'self'` has no nonce / `'strict-dynamic'`; while coexisting with SRI injection, all inline scripts are blocked | `done` | Root cause: the review mistakenly treated "no nonce/strict-dynamic" as required; the current pages only load external scripts and SRI works in parallel — there is no inline bootstrap being mistakenly blocked. |
| 354 | `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts:71-95` — `configurePreviewServer` sets CSP, `configureServer(dev)` does not; dev and production CSPs are inconsistent | `done` | Root cause: the dev server previously had no equivalent CSP injection; both dev and preview server paths now inject CSP uniformly. |
| 355 | `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts:101` — react-native alias uses `new URL(...).pathname`; on Windows this contains a leading `/` plus drive letter, alias fails | `done` | Root cause: the alias path resolution previously took `new URL().pathname` directly; it is now `fileURLToPath()`, compatible with Windows drive letters. |
| 356 | `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts:108` — `sourcemap:"hidden"` in production still generates sourcemap files, which can be reverse-engineered | `done` | Root cause: production builds previously still emitted hidden sourcemap; sourcemap output is now disabled in production. |
| 357 | `ui/apps/web/vite.config.ts` — no `define:` to exclude `process.env`; Node globals may be baked in | `done` | Root cause: Vite previously did not explicitly clear `process.env`; Node globals are now blocked from being baked in via `define`. |
| 358 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:9` — `cache.addAll(["/","/offline"])`, when the `/offline` resource 404s the entire install fails and the SW never activates | `done` | Root cause: the SW pre-cache mixed in the non-existent `/offline`; that resource has been removed, and install is no longer blocked by a 404. |
| 359 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:41` — every successful GET does `cache.put`, no LRU / capacity upper bound | `done` | Root cause: the static cache previously only did `cache.put` without capacity governance; a max-entry trim has been added. |
| 360 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:71-76` — `normalizeCacheRequest` removes `search`/`hash`; search-result pages / different queries share the cache, polluting it | `done` | Root cause: the old cache-key normalization stripped query/hash; the current implementation builds the key directly from the raw `Request`, no longer polluting different query pages. |
| 361 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:75` — `new Request(url,{method:"GET"})` discards original headers (e.g. `Accept-Language`); multiple locales share the same entry | `done` | Root cause: the old implementation lost headers when rebuilding `Request`; it now caches the original request directly, preserving variant headers like `Accept-Language`. |
| 362 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:96-107` — sequential `await` replay; one slow request blocks all; failed responses are not deleted and have no retry cap | `done` | Root cause: offline replay previously ran serially without a termination condition; it now replays in concurrent batches with a failure cap. |
| 363 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:18-22` — `activate` only deletes caches with the `aa-ui-runtime-` prefix; old caches from prior prefixes are left behind | `done` | Root cause: the activate phase previously only removed old caches by a single prefix; it now cleans up all historical `aa-ui-` caches while preserving the current shell cache. |
| 364 | `ui/apps/web/public/aa-sw.js:141-145` — the `transaction.oncomplete` check is always `undefined`, the logic always takes the early-resolve branch, resolves before awaiting complete — race | `done` | Root cause: the IndexedDB transaction wait was previously incorrectly resolved early; it now uniformly waits for `transaction.oncomplete` / `onerror` / `onabort`. |
| 365 | `apps/web/package.json` — only 2 deps, but actually imports 30+ `@aa/*` | `done` | Root cause: the review was based on the early minimal package manifest; the current web app explicitly declares actual dependencies such as shared/react/router — it is no longer "only 2 deps". |
| 366 | `apps/web/index.html` — missing meta description / icon / fallback text | `done` | Root cause: the old HTML skeleton lacked explanatory meta and loading fallback; `description`, icon, and loading text have been added. |

## ui/apps/electron-win

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 367 | `electron-win/package.json:9` — smoke directly runs `node ./src/index.ts`, no `--import tsx`/build, pure Node 22 must fail | `done` | Root cause: the smoke script previously executed TS with bare Node directly; it is now `node --import tsx ./src/index.ts`. |
| 368 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts:103` — `window.open` external links have no origin allowlist | `done` | Root cause: external link opening previously had no URL allowlist; it is now restricted to `https:` / `mailto:` and local-dev HTTP. |
| 369 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts:~162` — `globalShortcut` registered but not `unregisterAll` before quit; leaks across restarts | `done` | Root cause: global shortcut lifecycle previously had no quit cleanup; `unregisterAll()` is now called uniformly in `will-quit`. |
| 370 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/preload.ts:34-35` — bridge exposes both `AA_ELECTRON` and private `__AA_ELECTRON__`; the latter has no integrity check | `done` | Root cause: preload previously exposed the public and private bridges under two names; only the frozen single `AA_ELECTRON` object is now exposed. |
| 371 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/preload.ts` — IPC channels exposed by preload are not wired in `main.ts`; calls fail silently instead of returning a typed error | `done` | Root cause: preload's and main's IPC contracts were once out of sync; `ipcMain.handle` is now registered for every exposed channel. |
| 372 | `ui/apps/electron-win/package.json:7` — `main:"src/main.ts"`, but Electron does not parse TS and there is no `build:tsc` step; `electron .` fails to start | `done` | Root cause: the Electron package previously pointed `main` at the TS source and had no compile step; it now points to `dist/main.js` with a `tsc` build added. |
| 373 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — the module defines `bootstrapElectronShell` but the package entry never calls it, so `app.whenReady` is never triggered | `done` | Root cause: the main process module defined the bootstrap but had no direct entry wiring; the direct-entry branch now auto-calls `bootstrapElectronShell()`. |
| 374 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts:11,94` — `ALLOWED_SHELL_COMMANDS` / `isShellCommandAllowed` are exported but no IPC handler calls them; the allowlist is dead code | `done` | Root cause: the diagnostic-command allowlist was left as an unconsumed export early on; this dead code has been removed to avoid the "restricted shell" illusion. |
| 375 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — missing `app.requestSingleInstanceLock`; multiple starts spawn duplicate processes | `done` | Root cause: the desktop shell previously lacked a single-instance lock; `app.requestSingleInstanceLock()` is now requested at startup. |
| 376 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts:103-106` — `setWindowOpenHandler` `shell.openExternal(url)` accepts any URL without limiting protocol; `file://` / `javascript:` can be injected | `done` | Root cause: window external links previously had no protocol restriction; only allowlisted URLs are now permitted, with everything else uniformly denied. |
| 377 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — `webContents` does not register a `will-navigate` guard; renderer-layer redirects to external domains bypass the sandbox | `done` | Root cause: `webContents` previously lacked `will-navigate` protection; cross-origin navigation is now intercepted and routed through allowlisted external-open. |
| 378 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — no `session.defaultSession.webRequest` CSP header injection | `done` | Root cause: Electron response headers previously had no CSP injection; it is now injected via `session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived`. |
| 379 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — missing `autoUpdater` wiring (`package.json` depends on `electron-updater` but the update has no entry) | `done` | Root cause: the `electron-updater` dependency was declared but not wired; it is now dynamically imported at shell startup and `checkForUpdatesAndNotify()` runs. |
| 380 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/main.ts` — missing `app.on('window-all-closed')` handling; macOS exit semantics are wrong | `done` | Root cause: the desktop shell previously missed the `window-all-closed` platform semantics; the darwin exception and non-darwin exit logic have been added. |
| 381 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/preload.ts:34-35` — `AA_ELECTRON` and `__AA_ELECTRON__` share the same object reference; if the renderer overwrites one, the other is also polluted | `done` | Root cause: the old preload double-exposure caused both global names to share the same reference; it is now closed to a single frozen bridge, with no coupled-pollution surface. |
| 382 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/preload.ts:27` — `installElectronBridge(target,bridge)` first parameter `target:Window` is a placeholder (`void target`), misleading API | `done` | Root cause: `installElectronBridge(target, ...)` previously treated `target` as a placeholder parameter; the current implementation actually installs the bridge onto the passed `target` when `contextBridge` is absent. |
| 383 | `ui/apps/electron-win/src/renderer.js:1-43` — desktop splash is all-English hardcoded, no i18n / RTL, disconnected from the web main shell | `done` | Root cause: the Electron fallback copy previously only had English constants; it now outputs Chinese/English localized copy based on `document.lang`. |
| 384 | `electron-win/renderer.js` — hand-written DOM placeholder, does not load the React main app | `done` | Root cause: the review was based on the old state of only having a fallback shell; the current main window prefers to load `../../web/dist/index.html`, with hand-written DOM only as a fallback shell when the web build is missing. |
| 385 | `electron-win/index.html` — "Electron Windows Shell Baseline" placeholder copy is delivered as-is | `done` | Root cause: the deliverable page title used to be a baseline placeholder; it is now the formal product title and loading text. |



| 386 | `electron-win/package.json` — `electron@^42.1.0` does not exist | `done` | Root cause: the review record stayed at the non-existent `electron@^42.1.0`; the current package version is now the installable `^31.0.0`. |
| 387 | `electron-win/main.ts:9` — `rendererHtmlPath = "../dist/index.html"`, but the package has no build script that produces this file; production startup 404s | `done` | Root cause: Electron build previously only compiled TS, not copying the fallback shell resources; the build now produces `dist/index.html` and `dist/renderer.js`, and the main-process fallback path is no longer dangling. |
| 388 | `electron-win/main.ts:118-126` — `globalShortcut.register`'s return value is discarded; conflicts are silent | `done` | Root cause: shortcut registration previously only called `globalShortcut.register()` without consuming the return value; registration failures are now explicitly logged. |
| 389 | `electron-win/main.ts:159-164` — missing `will-quit`/`window-all-closed` and `globalShortcut.unregisterAll()`; OS-level shortcuts leak | `done` | Root cause: the desktop shell early on did not include shortcut unregistration in its lifecycle; unregistration is now done in `will-quit`, and the `window-all-closed` exit semantics are filled in. |
| 390 | `electron-win/preload.ts:34-35` — exposes the bridge under both `AA_ELECTRON` and `__AA_ELECTRON__` names | `done` | Root cause: preload previously kept a legacy private alias; the surface is now closed to a single frozen `AA_ELECTRON` bridge. |
| 391 | `electron-win/preload.ts:27-28` — `installElectronBridge(target,...)` immediately `void target;` discards the parameter | `done` | Root cause: the preload install function originally treated `target` as a placeholder parameter; when `contextBridge` is absent the bridge is now actually installed onto the passed window object. |
| 392 | `electron-win/index.html:8` — Electron CSP lacks `worker-src` and `report-uri` | `done` | Root cause: the Electron shell's CSP only covered the most basic directives; `worker-src` and `report-uri` have been added and synchronized to the main process response header injection. |
| 393 | `electron-win/package.json:15-19` — `build.win.signAndEditExecutable: true` but the repo has no signing config, CI must fail | `done` | Root cause: the Windows packaging config kept a switch requiring signing material that the repo does not provide; `signAndEditExecutable` is now off by default. |
| 394 | `ui/eslint.config.js:25` — does not ignore `apps/electron-win/dist/**` and Tauri build products | `done` | Root cause: the lint ignore list only covered `web dist`, not Electron/Tauri product directories; ignore rules for desktop shell build products have been added. |

## ui/apps/tauri-*

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 395 | `ui/apps/tauri-linux/src/index.ts` — same adapter-per-render anti-pattern as mobile | `done` | Root cause: this review entry mistakenly attributed a React-shell problem to a pure adapter-factory file; `src/index.ts` only exposes the adapter factory and manifest, with no render-time adapter rebuild. |
| 396 | `ui/apps/tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:16` — CSP `img-src 'self' data: https: https:` wildcard is equivalent to any external site; missing `worker-src`/`font-src`/`media-src` | `done` | Root cause: the Linux Tauri CSP previously lazily opened up the `https:` wildcard and missed `worker`/`font`/`media`; it has been tightened to local resources with the missing directives added. |
| 397 | `tauri-macos/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:30` — `pubkey: "macos-demo-public-key"` placeholder, updater accepts forged updates | `done` | Root cause: the macOS updater stayed in "placeholder wiring" state but was still enabled by default; the updater is now disabled by default to avoid shipping with a fake public key. |
| 398 | `tauri-macos/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:21`, `tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:23` — updater endpoint `automatic-agent.example` fake TLD | `done` | Root cause: the desktop shell config once hardcoded example domains as publishing endpoints; the default updater is now disabled and placeholder endpoints removed. |
| 399 | `tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` — no `pubkey` field, signature validation is not configured | `done` | Root cause: the Linux updater was previously enabled by default but had no signature-validation config; the updater is now disabled by default to avoid exposing an unsigned channel. |
| 400 | `tauri-macos/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:33` — `plugins.shell.open: true` with no scope whitelist | `done` | Root cause: the Tauri shell capability was previously wide open; `shell.open` is now off by default. |
| 401 | `ui/apps/tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:26-34` — `shell.open:true`, `os.all:true`, `notification.all:true` overly broad capabilities | `done` | Root cause: Linux Tauri capabilities originally used an "all available" placeholder; `shell`/`os`/`notification` are now tightened by default. |
| 402 | `ui/apps/tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:20-25` — updater has no `pubkey`, Linux updater does not verify signatures | `done` | Root cause: the Linux updater config was in a half-wired state, with endpoint but no pubkey; the updater is now disabled by default. |
| 403 | `ui/apps/tauri-linux/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:23` — updater endpoint `.example` placeholder points to a dead domain | `done` | Root cause: the Linux updater used an example domain placeholder; the default endpoint is cleared and the updater is disabled. |
| 404 | `tauri-macos/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:25` — `pubkey:"macos-demo-public-key"` placeholder, signature verification must fail | `done` | Root cause: the macOS updater previously kept a demo public key; the updater is now disabled by default instead of shipping with a fake key. |
| 405 | `tauri-macos/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:31-39` — same overly broad `os.all`/`shell.open`/`notification.all` as Linux | `done` | Root cause: the macOS Tauri config, like Linux, used the wide-open capability placeholder; it has been tightened in lockstep. |

## ui/apps/mobile

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 406 | `apps/mobile/src/App.tsx:13` — `createMobilePlatformAdapter(detectPlatform())` without `useMemo`; rebuilds the adapter on every render | `done` | Root cause: the mobile shell previously created the adapter directly during render; platform detection and adapter construction are now both wrapped in `useMemo`. |
| 407 | `apps/mobile/src/App.tsx:8` — `detectPlatform()` relies on `navigator.userAgent`; in a pure RN environment it falls through to `android` | `done` | Root cause: platform detection originally only used browser UA; it now prefers React Native `Platform.OS` and falls back to UA. |
| 408 | `apps/mobile/src/App.tsx:19,20` — `mobileNavigation.tabs[0]!`, `settingsSubRoutes[0]!` non-null assertions with no fallback UI | `done` | Root cause: the mobile shell previously assumed navigation config was never empty; non-null assertions have been removed and an explicit fallback UI added. |
| 409 | `ui/apps/mobile/src/App.tsx:13` — `createMobilePlatformAdapter()` inlined in render without `useMemo`; rebuilds the adapter on every render | `done` | Root cause: same as #406, the adapter creation logic was inlined in the component body; it is now memoized. |
| 410 | `apps/mobile/metro.config.js:1` — CJS syntax but parent `package.json:5` is `"type":"module"` | `done` | Root cause: Metro config used the CommonJS template, but the workspace has switched to ESM; the config is now an ESM file. |
| 411 | `apps/mobile/app.json:1-4` — only `name`/`displayName`; missing `expo`/`scheme`/`version`/`orientation` | `done` | Root cause: the mobile app manifest only had minimal placeholder fields; Expo base metadata has been added. |
| 412 | `ui/apps/mobile/app.json:1-4` — only `name`/`displayName`; missing `expo`/iOS `bundleIdentifier`/Android `package`/`icons`/`permissions`, cannot build for release | `done` | Root cause: release-side mobile metadata had long been missing; iOS/Android identifiers, icon placeholders, and permission declarations have been added. |
| 413 | `ui/apps/mobile/package.json:11` — only a smoke script, missing `start`/`android`/`ios`/`build`; `react-native` is a dependency but Metro bundler dependency is not declared | `done` | Root cause: the mobile workspace only kept a smoke placeholder script, with no real dev/packaging commands; `start`/`android`/`ios`/`build` scripts and Metro dependency have been added. |
| 414 | `ui/apps/mobile/metro.config.js:11` — `unstable_enablePackageExports:true` but monorepo packages do not declare `exports` field, runtime resolution fails | `done` | Root cause: Metro's resolver used to enable the package-exports mode too early, but the monorepo did not fully declare `exports`; that switch is now off by default. |

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |

| 415 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/index.tsx:53-66` — `createFeatureModule`'s `Component` only has a `try/catch` around sync render errors; async errors inside hooks are not caught | `done` | Root cause: the feature module previously used a function-level `try/catch` to impersonate an error boundary; it is now a real React error boundary wrapping the feature subtree. |
| 416 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/FeatureScaffold.stories.tsx` — only 1 `Basic` story; 30+ components (Card/Panel/Tabs/Drawer/Accordion/Stepper/PieChart, etc.) have zero stories | `done` | Root cause: Storybook only kept the minimal `FeatureScaffold` story; extended component and chart stories have been added, covering interaction primitives and data visualization baselines. |
| 417 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:217-219` — `Tooltip` only sets `title` + `aria-label` on a `<span>` with no keyboard focusable target, so it cannot be triggered on hover | `done` | Root cause: the tooltip container was previously not focusable; the wrapper is now focusable so keyboard users can also trigger the tooltip. |
| 418 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:221-228` — `Drawer` has no focus trap / ESC close / overlay / focus return, violating dialog WAI-ARIA | `done` | Root cause: `Drawer` was originally just a fixed-positioned side-panel container; focus trap, ESC, overlay, and focus return have been added. |
| 419 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:233-236` — `Toast` `role="status"` and `aria-live="assertive"` conflict (role / live-region double-directive) | `done` | Root cause: `Toast` previously rendered the danger state as `status`; the danger state is now `alert`, while the rest stays `status`/`polite`. |
| 420 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:166-173` — `Pagination` directly renders one button per `totalPages`; 10^4 pages blow up the DOM | `done` | Root cause: the pagination control used to expand one button per page; it is now windowed pagination with ellipsis. |
| 421 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:185-196` — `Tabs` lacks left/right arrow-key navigation / `aria-controls` / `tabindex` management | `done` | Root cause: `Tabs` previously only had click switching; arrow keys, `aria-controls`, and roving `tabindex` have been added. |
| 422 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:199-215` — `Accordion` buttons lack `aria-controls` pointing to content; the content `<div>` has no `id` / `role=region` | `done` | Root cause: `Accordion` previously only toggled display state without semantic association; the trigger/panel now have an `id` and `region` relationship. |
| 423 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:329-353` — `SegmentedControl` `role=radiogroup` but no arrow-key navigation or roving `tabindex` | `done` | Root cause: segmented control previously only declared the radiogroup semantics without implementing keyboard behavior; arrow keys and roving `tabindex` have been added. |
| 424 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:439-453` — `formatRemainingDuration` with `new Date(deadline)` returns `NaN` for invalid strings, outputting `"NaNm remaining"` | `done` | Root cause: the SLA countdown previously trusted any string to be a valid time; invalid times now return an explicit fallback message. |
| 425 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:117-127` — `Skeleton` uses `aria-hidden` but has no motion animation, so the loading state is not perceivable for low-vision users | `done` | Root cause: the skeleton used to be a static gradient block; a shimmer animation has been added. |
| 426 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:8-9,16` — `StatusPill` text color hardcoded to `#04130a`; after theme switch contrast cannot guarantee WCAG AA | `done` | Root cause: the status pill color was previously hardcoded in the component; it now uses the design-token text color instead of a fixed hex. |
| 427 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:265-272` — `Stepper` only marks the active step with `aria-current="step"`; other steps lack `aria-disabled`/`aria-current="false"` and there is no `role="list"` relationship | `done` | Root cause: `Stepper` was previously just a visual list; list/listitem relationships and current/disabled semantics have been added. |
| 428 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:195` — `FeatureWorkbench.onChange:(event:Event)=>...` uses DOM `Event` instead of React `ChangeEvent` | `done` | Root cause: the workbench's input event type was previously the native DOM `Event`; it is now the React synthetic event type. |
| 429 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:238` — `onKeyDown:(event:KeyboardEvent)=>` uses a DOM type instead of a React synthetic event | `done` | Root cause: the workbench's keyboard event type was also written as a DOM `KeyboardEvent`; it is now unified to React keyboard events. |
| 430 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:153-160` — `triggerAction` awaits `action.onTrigger?.(item)` without `try/catch`; user callbacks throwing become unhandled rejections | `done` | Root cause: workbench action previously directly awaited external callbacks; structured failure capture has been added to avoid unhandled rejections. |
| 431 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:46-52` — `KeyValueTable` uses `key:row.key` as the React key; two rows with the same key collide | `done` | Root cause: `KeyValueTable` previously assumed business keys were globally unique; it is now a key+index composite key. |
| 432 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:138` — `filter.toLowerCase()` rather than `toLocaleLowerCase`; Turkish `i`/`I` locale folding fails | `done` | Root cause: workbench filter logic previously only did ASCII lowercasing; it now uses locale-aware lowercasing. |
| 433 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:230-269` — `Workbench` list uses `<button role=option>`; listbox lacks `aria-activedescendant` / single tabstop | `done` | Root cause: the workbench list previously made every option its own button; it is now a single tabstop listbox + `aria-activedescendant` pattern. |
| 434 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/index.ts:288` — `aria-relevant="additions text"` is missing `removals`; activity log deletes are not announced | `done` | Root cause: the activity log live region previously only announced additions; `removals` is now included in `aria-relevant`. |
| 435 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:99-101` — `"mock" in ResizeObserver` heuristic; a real class with a `mock` field is mis-routed to the callable path | `done` | Root cause: the `ResizeObserver` construction branch previously relied on a `mock` field heuristic; that branch has been removed, leaving only the controlled constructor fallback. |
| 436 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:119,123` — `useMemo` dependency array contains variable-length `...chartColorDeps`, violating the React hooks static-dependency contract | `done` | Root cause: the chart option memo previously spread a variable-length dependency array; it is now an explicit static dependency list. |
| 437 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:153` — init `useEffect` dependencies `[]` but the closure captures `theme`/`buildChartOption`; runtime theme switch does not rebuild the chart | `done` | Root cause: the review was concerned that theme updates would not take effect; the current implementation has folded theme changes into the option-update effect, so runtime re-skinning re-`setOption`s. |
| 438 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:166-172` — `setOption` has already replaced the data, then `appendData` repeats the tail, producing ghosted series | `done` | Root cause: append-only updates previously did `setOption` on all data, then appended the tail; in append mode the old series is now only `setOption`-ed, and the new tail is appended. |
| 439 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:128-129` — `userAgent.includes("jsdom")` UA sniff, custom UAs cause misjudgment and skip init | `done` | Root cause: chart init previously skipped jsdom via UA sniffing; that string sniff has been removed. |
| 440 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface-runtime.tsx:138` — `addEventListener("resize")` bound to `defaultView` at mount time, when the container moves to a new window the resize no longer fires | `done` | Root cause: chart size update previously also relied on a window resize listener at mount; it is now a container-level `ResizeObserver`. |
| 441 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/index.tsx:160` — `HeatmapGrid` color hardcoded to `rgba(34,197,94,a)`, cannot follow theme changes | `done` | Root cause: heatmap color was previously hardcoded to green RGBA; it now uses the design-token accent color with dynamic transparency. |
| 442 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/index.tsx:80-81` — `ScatterPlot` only takes `maxX`/`maxY`; negative values and zero-baseline scatter points escape the viewBox | `done` | Root cause: scatter coordinate mapping previously only normalized by max; it is now normalized across the min/max range. |
| 443 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/index.tsx:63` — `BarChart` directly stuffs the external `point.tone` string into `background`, with no CSS value whitelist | `done` | Root cause: bar chart previously treated external `tone` as any CSS value and put it straight into style; it is now an allowlist mapping. |
| 444 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/index.tsx:55,83,111,140` — chart uses `role="img"` with an `aria-label`, hiding the data without providing an expandable SR text table | `done` | Root cause: lightweight charts previously only exposed a summary `aria-label`; bar/scatter/gauge/heatmap now have expandable data tables. |
| 445 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/index.tsx:142` — `<span />` empty placeholder without `aria-hidden`, screen readers read the empty cell | `done` | Root cause: the heatmap top-left empty placeholder cell previously had no `aria-hidden`; it is now explicitly hidden. |
| 446 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/charts/echart-surface.tsx:11` — `lazy(()=>import(...))` with no error boundary wrapping, Suspense fallback does not handle chunk load failure | `done` | Root cause: the EChart lazy runtime previously only had a Suspense fallback, with no chunk-load error boundary; a runtime error boundary has been added. |
| 447 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/layouts/index.ts:26` — `ThreePaneLayout` accepts `viewportWidth` prop but does not use it at all (lines 26-41), dead parameter | `done` | Root cause: the three-pane layout had a leftover unused `viewportWidth` placeholder; the dead parameter has been removed to avoid misleading the API. |
| 448 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/layouts/index.ts:25-41` — three panes have no `aside`/`main`/`aside` landmark, all `<div>`, no `aria-label` | `done` | Root cause: the early layout only did the visual grid, without mapping information architecture to landmarks; it is now `aside`/`main`/`aside` with `aria-label`. |
| 449 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:401-408` — `PieChart` `gradientStops` accumulates percent as float; 1000+ slices cause precision drift and cracks | `done` | Root cause: pie chart gradient start/end percentages used to keep cutting and summing, accumulating float error; it now sums once and closes the last slice to `100%`. |
| 450 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:411-422` — `PieChart` only has `aria-label="Pie chart"` and no `role`; screen readers do not read slice details | `done` | Root cause: pie chart previously only had a coarse label; `role="img"` title/description association has been added, and screen readers can read slice details. |
| 451 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:524-541` — `DAGVisualization` `repeat(stages.length,1fr)` single-row layout, with 20+ stages column width is below the readable threshold and there is no horizontal scroll | `done` | Root cause: DAG card width was previously squeezed linearly in a single-row equal-column layout; it is now min-column-width + horizontal scroll, so long pipelines are readable. |
| 452 | `ui/packages/ui-core/src/components/extended.tsx:265-272` — `Stepper` uses `<ol>` but inner `<li>` has no `role`/link, keyboard tab skips the whole sequence | `done` | Root cause: `Stepper` previously only kept the visual sequence without stable focusable semantics; list-item semantics have been added and reached steps are now tab-accessible. |
| 453 | `ui/.storybook/main.ts:5` — stories glob only scans `packages/ui-core/**`; all `packages/features/*` and `packages/shared/ui/*` stories have zero coverage | `done` | Root cause: Storybook config only covered `ui-core`; it has been extended to include `features/*` and `shared/*` stories. |

## ui/packages/shared/api-client (rest, ws, interceptors)

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 454 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/rest-client.ts:~398` — `fallbackTransport` silently returns mock after 401/403 retry failure, hiding auth failure | `done` | Root cause: fallback logic previously did not distinguish HTTP semantic errors from network errors; fallback is now only allowed for non-`RestHttpError` cases and no longer swallows auth failures. |
| 455 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/rest-client.ts:255-327` — retry loop treats 4xx as transient, invalid retries amplify rate-limit | `done` | Root cause: retry judgment once treated client errors as recoverable; only `429`/`5xx` and retryable requests are now allowed. |
| 456 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/rest-client.ts` — default `credentials:"include"` on cross-origin requests, CSRF / cookie leak | `done` | Root cause: HTTP transport's default was too wide; it now defaults to `same-origin`, no longer automatically carrying cross-origin cookies. |
| 457 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/rest-client.ts` — directly `crypto.randomUUID()` without a `globalThis` guard, fails in jsdom / old Node | `done` | Root cause: request envelope id directly depended on `crypto.randomUUID()`; it now uniformly uses the fallback-equipped `generateStableId()`. |
| 458 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:49` — `createTraceInterceptor` likewise `crypto.randomUUID()` without fallback | `done` | Root cause: trace id generation, like transport, was hard-bound to browser crypto; the stable id generator is now reused so test environments can run. |
| 459 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:243` — `createRetryInterceptor` retries on every error (including 4xx / `AbortError`), no status allowlist | `done` | Root cause: interceptor-level retry previously lacked error-type and idempotency boundaries; `4xx`/`AbortError` are now excluded and only safely retryable requests go through. |
| 460 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:274` — `createDedupeInterceptor` has module-level singleton state, leaks across vitest files | `done` | Root cause: dedupe observation state was once over-scoped; state is now encapsulated only in the interceptor instance and cleared after responses, with no cross-instance leaks. |
| 461 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:294` — dedupe key uses `JSON.stringify(body)`; unstable object key order causes cache invalidation | `done` | Root cause: dedupe key previously did `JSON.stringify` directly; different object key orders were treated as different requests; it is now stable serialization. |
| 462 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:188` — `createOfflineQueueInterceptor` also enqueues `HEAD`/`OPTIONS`, causing a replay storm | `done` | Root cause: offline-enqueue conditions previously only excluded `GET`; `HEAD`/`OPTIONS` are now explicitly excluded to avoid meaningless replays. |
| 463 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/interceptors.ts:294` vs `312` — dedupe key format differs at two sites, cross-site lookup never matches | `done` | Root cause: dedupe key was built separately at `onRequest` and `intercept`; both now share the same `buildKey()`. |
| 464 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/ws-client.ts:48` — `eventId` regex `^evt[-_][A-Za-z0-9:-]{1,}$` is narrower than the contract, valid ids are dropped at line 252 | `done` | Root cause: replay event id validation was too narrow; the allowed character set is now widened to the contract-allowed set and legitimate events are no longer mistakenly dropped. |
| 465 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/ws-client.ts:218` — token carried via `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol` sub-protocol, recorded by proxy / access logs | `done` | Root cause: early WS auth design once confused the token with the sub-protocol; the sub-protocol name is now fixed, and the token is only sent in the first auth message. |
| 466 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/ws-client.ts:336` — reconnect jitter uses `Math.random()` with no injectable seed, tests are not reproducible | `done` | Root cause: reconnect backoff was previously hard-bound to `Math.random()`; the random source is now injected via options, making tests reproducible. |
| 467 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/shared-ws-worker.ts:207` — `self.onconnect=…` runs at module top-level; any import installs the handler on the wrong global | `done` | Root cause: the SharedWorker runtime previously installed the handler directly at module top-level; it is now an explicit install function with a runtime environment guard. |
| 468 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/shared-ws-worker.ts:172` — `reconnectTimer` does not null out the old `setTimeout` before scheduling a new one, timers can stack | `done` | Root cause: the worker reconnect timer was not reset after the callback fired; it now nulls first and then reconnects, avoiding timer stacking. |
| 469 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/ws-event-router.ts:74` — `subscribe` does not dedupe handlers, duplicate registration triggers twice | `done` | Root cause: the event router previously only appended cleanup, without deduping by channel; the subscription table is now maintained per channel. |
| 470 | `ui/packages/shared/api-client/src/ws-event-router.ts` — `disconnect()` does not clear the listener registry, after reconnect routes to the last ghost handler | `done` | Root cause: on disconnect the code only called dispose, not clearing the internal registry; `disconnect` now also clears the channel cleanup table. |
| 471 | `04-runtime-sequence.md:145` — references `ui/packages/shared/api-client/...` that needs review | `done` | Root cause: the runtime-sequence doc only listed endpoint landing points, missing `interceptors/rest/ws/router`; references have been added. |
| 472 | `ui/vitest.config.ts` — jsdom environment does not polyfill `crypto.randomUUID` / `crypto.subtle`; interceptors/ws-client imported immediately crash in unit tests | `done` | Root cause: the test bootstrap file did not supply browser crypto capabilities; `ui/tests/setup.ts` now backs up `webcrypto`/`randomUUID`. |
| 473 | `ui/tests/shared/api-client.test.ts:193` — mutates `document.head.innerHTML`, `QueryClient` is never released, leaking timers across specs | `done` | Root cause: the shared API test previously polluted `document.head` without unified teardown; the suite now explicitly cleans up DOM-side state after end. |
| 474 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/shared/ws-client.test.ts:158` — depends on `setTimeout(…,10)` to order assertions, slow CI causes flakes | `done` | Root cause: the WS unit test used to rely on real time slices for ordering; it is now fake timers / tick driven, so CI is no longer fragile. |
| 475 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/shared/ws-client.test.ts:269` — within a single `it`, `vi.spyOn(Math,"random")` has no per-test `mockRestore`; subsequent `it`s inherit the spy until `afterEach` | `done` | Root cause: the test previously spied on `Math.random()` globally; `BrowserWSClient` now takes a random source via injection, no longer polluting the global. |
| 476 | `shared/api-client` interceptor combination — `createIdempotencyKeyInterceptor` is registered earlier than `createRetryInterceptor`; on retry the idempotency key is regenerated, bypassing server-side dedupe | `done` | Root cause: the review was based on an old retry assumption; the current idempotency key is generated once at `onRequest` time, and the same request/header is reused for retries — the key is not regenerated. |

## ui/packages/shared/auth & token

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 477 | `ui/packages/shared/auth/src/auth-service.ts:121-126` — `handleSsoCallback` always throws `"auth.redirecting"`; the documented happy path is dead code | `done` | Root cause: the SSO callback entry previously only kept the fail-close redirect; the code/state callback is now delegated to the PKCE token exchange, and the happy path is reachable. |
| 478 | `ui/packages/shared/auth/src/auth-service.ts` — SSO callback does not clear the fragment from `window.history` after parsing, leaving the token in browser history / back stack | `done` | Root cause: callback cleanup was missing; the callback now uniformly does `history.replaceState()` to clear the `code`/`token`/`error` parameters. |
| 479 | `ui/packages/shared/auth/src/token-manager.ts` — access / refresh tokens written in plaintext to `localStorage`; an XSS leaks them | `done` | Root cause: session persistence boundary was once confused with token management; the current `TokenManager` stays in memory, and the auth store's persistence has also dropped access / refresh tokens. |
| 480 | `api-auth-service.ts:228-231` — `verificationSecrets.some(...)` short-circuited comparison leaks timing | `done` | Root cause: the review pointed at a now-removed old `api-auth-service.ts` implementation; the current `shared/auth` path no longer has that short-circuit comparison surface. |
| 481 | `shared/auth` (`AuthSession`) vs `shared/state/auth-store` (`AuthStoreState`) — two session model field sets overlap, drift silently | `done` | Root cause: `shared/auth` and `shared/state` previously maintained their own session fields; the auth store's session type is now directly aligned with the `shared/auth` session model. |

## ui/packages/shared/sync

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 482 | `ui/packages/shared/sync/src/offline-queue.ts:82` — `trimToCapacity` silently drops the oldest on overflow with no telemetry / DLQ / caller signal | `done` | Root cause: the offline queue previously directly `shift()`-ed on overflow; it now supports an `onEvict` callback to expose the drop event to callers / telemetry. |
| 483 | `ui/packages/shared/sync/src/sync-coordinator.ts:107` — replay does not re-attach `Authorization` / `X-CSRF-Token` / `Idempotency-Key` / `tenant` headers; refresh causes 401/403 storms | `done` | Root cause: the offline-mutation type previously did not retain the protected request headers; the required headers are now persisted on enqueue and fully replayed on flush. |
| 484 | `ui/packages/shared/sync/src/conflict-resolver.ts:137` — `preferMostRecent` falls back to `localValue` when timestamps are missing / equal, contrary to the documented `server_wins` default | `done` | Root cause: the fallback strategy previously leaned toward local when metadata was missing; it now takes local only when local is strictly newer, otherwise falling back to server. |
| 485 | `ui/packages/shared/sync/src/conflict-resolver.ts` — does not validate `lastModified`; forged future timestamps win every time | `done` | Root cause: timestamp comparison previously had no reasonable clock-drift upper bound; future timestamps are now considered invalid and no longer win automatically. |
| 486 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/shared/sync-coordinator.test.ts:11` — hardcoded real future date `2026-05-01T00:00:00.000Z`; `Date.now()`-style assertions drift with the clock | `done` | Root cause: the relevant sync regression cases used to depend on calendar-drifting time assumptions; the assertions are now fixed semantic checks, no longer bound to the real clock. |

## ui/packages/shared/state (stores, query, mutations)

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 487 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/middleware.ts:19` — `cloneDraftValue` uses JSON-style clone, dropping `Map` / `Set` / `Symbol` keys / class instances | `done` | Root cause: draft clone previously only covered arrays and plain objects; it now prefers `structuredClone` and adds `Map`/`Set`/`Reflect.ownKeys` branches. |
| 488 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/auth-store.ts:90` — `logout()` does `Object.assign(draft, DEFAULT_AUTH_STATE)`, sharing array references with the default object, so subsequent mutations alias the default | `done` | Root cause: the auth default state was previously a shared object constant; it is now a factory that generates a new default each time, so logout always gets a fresh copy. |
| 489 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/auth-store.ts:60` — persistence key `aa-auth-store` contains plaintext access / refresh tokens | `done` | Root cause: auth-store persistence previously dumped the full session; `partialize` now strips access / refresh tokens. |
| 490 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/sync-store.ts:54` — `setPendingMutations` resets the counter but keeps `syncing` / `error` state | `done` | Root cause: pending count and sync state previously lacked a unified convergence rule; when pending goes to zero the state is now re-folded based on conflict / error / idle. |
| 491 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/sync-store.ts:88` — `resolveConflict`'s `"merge"` branch silently no-ops | `done` | Root cause: single-conflict resolution was once mis-modeled as a global strategy side-effect; `local` / `server` / `merge` now only resolve the current conflict and clear error state. |
| 492 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/sync-store.ts:94-99` — a single conflict resolution overwrites the global `strategy`, leaking policy across unrelated conflicts | `done` | Root cause: per-conflict resolution previously directly overwrote the global strategy; that side-effect has been removed, and the strategy can only be set explicitly. |
| 493 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/notification-store.ts:33` — `generateId` uses `Date.now()+Math.random()`; sudden collisions and tests are not reproducible | `done` | Root cause: the notification id previously depended on timestamp + random; it now uniformly uses the stable id generator, reducing collisions and being testable. |
| 494 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/realtime-store.ts:55` — `triggerPanic` has no inverse and the store persists it, so panic state survives reload | `done` | Root cause: panic previously had no clear action and was persisted; `clearPanic()` has been added and the persisted value is forced to `false`. |
| 495 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/query-client.ts:11` — factory named incorrectly and default `retry:3` lets TanStack Query retry 4xx | `done` | Root cause: the query client previously only had `createQueryClientFactory()` with default `retry:3`; a `createQueryClient()` alias has been added, and retries are now bounded to `429`/`5xx`. |
| 496 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/query-cache-persistence.ts:163` — `flush` ignores in-flight writes; subscriptions burst `setTimeout` reset but the prior write is still pending, causing mutual overwrites | `done` | Root cause: query-cache persistence previously had no write serialization; it now uses a `writeChain` to write to disk sequentially, avoiding concurrent overwrites. |
| 497 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/query-cache-persistence.ts:146` — `hydrate` has no `try/catch`; a corrupt IndexedDB crashes rather than falling back to a new cache | `done` | Root cause: `hydrate` previously assumed the persisted snapshot is always readable; reads now fail close and clear the bad cache. |
| 498 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/query-cache-persistence.ts` — any cache (including PII) is dropped into IndexedDB with no scrubbing / encryption | `done` | Root cause: query cache previously had no persistence allowlist; only safe query keys may now be persisted, and sensitive caches are not persisted by default. |
| 499 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/use-mutation.ts:81` — user `onMutate(variables, {} as QueryClient)` casts an empty object, cache calls crash | `done` | Root cause: the mutation hook previously passed a fake `QueryClient` to `onMutate`; it now passes a real instance via `useQueryClient()`. |
| 500 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/use-mutation.ts:88-91` — `onError` only fires when `context?.previousData` is truthy; errors are swallowed when `onMutate` returns `undefined` | `done` | Root cause: error callback previously incorrectly relied on `previousData` truthiness; `onError` is now forwarded regardless of whether the snapshot is empty. |
| 501 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/use-mutation.ts:62-66` — `client.post/put/patch(resolvedPath, variables)` sends the entire `variables` (including path params like `taskId`) as the body | `done` | Root cause: mutation body previously transparently passed `variables`; an explicit `body` is now supported, with `id`/`*Id` fields already in the path stripped by default. |
| 502 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/optimistic-update.ts:54` — `snapshotCache` is declared `async` but performs no awaited work, misleading API | `done` | Root cause: `snapshotCache()` was previously incorrectly declared `async`; it is now a sync API, matching its implementation. |
| 503 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/optimistic-update.ts:84` — `patchCache` cancels the query but does not auto-snapshot, so a caller that forgets to snapshot breaks rollback | `done` | Root cause: optimistic patch previously did not return a pre-state snapshot; `patchCache()` now captures the snapshot first and then updates the cache. |
| 504 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/mutations/optimistic-update.ts:129` — same `previousData` gating bug as `use-mutation` | `done` | Root cause: optimistic mutation options shared the same gating mistake as the hook; both now unconditionally forward `onError`. |
| 505 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/*` — persisted stores have no schema migration; any field shape change makes hydrate crash, forcing users to clear `localStorage` | `done` | Root cause: the persist middleware was long unversioned; the middleware now defaults to injecting `version`/`migrate`, and key stores have explicit migration config. |
| 506 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/auth-store.ts` — missing cross-tab `storage` event listener, so another tab still holds the old token after logout | `done` | Root cause: auth store previously only persisted within the current tab, not listening for cross-tab changes; the `storage` event is now wired to sync logout and identity. |
| 507 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/query-client.ts` — no `defaultOptions.queries.staleTime`; all queries go stale immediately, causing repeated request storms across dashboard hooks | `done` | Root cause: the query cache layered defaults were missing; `staleTime` / `gcTime` and retry boundaries have been added and the review doc state was written back. |
| 508 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/realtime-store.ts` — persistence + `triggerPanic` with no panic reset API; after offline reconnect the UI is still in panic | `done` | Root cause: panic state was designed as one-way trigger and persisted; `clearPanic` has been added and persisting `panicActivated` is forbidden. |
| 509 | `ui/packages/shared/state/src/stores/notification-store.ts:33` — `generateId=Date.now()+Math.random()` used as the React key, collisions cause same-frame notifications to mis-reuse DOM | `done` | Root cause: notification primary key used an unstable timestamp+random; it is now `generateStableId` and the review state has been written back. |

## ui/packages/shared/i18n

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 510 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:~185` — `setLocale` mutates `documentElement.lang`/`dir` at module init, polluting DOM across jsdom test files | `done` | Root cause: the i18n default instance directly mutated `document` at construction; the init-time DOM mutation has been removed, and only explicit `apply` writes to the document. |
| 511 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts` — `sharedTranslationService` is exported as a singleton with no `reset`; tests need to touch module internals | `done` | Root cause: the shared translation service lacked a lifecycle exit; `resetSharedTranslationService` / `dispose` have been added for tests and rebuilds. |
| 512 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:142` — translation-miss falls back to the key, UI displays `ui.feature.xxx.title` with no telemetry | `done` | Root cause: missing translations were treated as normal fallback; a diagnostics reporter has been added to explicitly surface misses and format errors. |
| 513 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:139` — `IntlMessageFormat.format(values) as string` cast; with selectors like `<b>{name}</b>` the return is an array and the type mismatches | `done` | Root cause: ICU format results were directly cast to `string`; explicit normalization of the format output now supports array fragments. |
| 514 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:139` — each `translate` does `new IntlMessageFormat(...)` with no hot cache, causing GC storms | `done` | Root cause: ICU formatters were not reused; a per-locale+message `formatterCache` has been introduced. |
| 515 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:185` — `setLocale("zh-CN")` is the default and force-initializes document mutation, default-locking `zh-CN` instead of user preference | `done` | Root cause: the default locale was hardcoded to `zh-CN`; detection now prefers the navigator preference and no longer force-locks Chinese. |
| 516 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:128-131` — fallback chain does not dedupe `locale + catalog.fallbackLocales + fallbackLocale`; repeated values cause the same catalog to be accessed multiple times | `done` | Root cause: fallback chain construction pushed to an array directly; it is now deduplicated with a `Set` before resolution. |
| 517 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:153` — `locale.split("-")[0]` produces `""` for empty input; `detectLocale` degrades to prefix-equals empty string | `done` | Root cause: the locale base language segment was not validated for empty; `detectLocale` now skips empty `baseLanguage`. |
| 518 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:206-218` — `translateFeatureCopy` calls `translateMessage` twice per `featureId`; N features → 2N `IntlMessageFormat` creations | `done` | Root cause: feature copy was resolved key-by-key through the full chain; `translateMany` now shares the lookup chain and reuses the formatter cache. |
| 519 | `ui/packages/shared/i18n/src/index.ts:111-118` — `applyLocaleToDocument` mutates `documentElement.lang`/`dir`; not cleaned up on `dispose` | `done` | Root cause: document `lang`/`dir` writes had no rollback snapshot; the old values are now recorded and restored in `dispose`. |

## ui/packages/shared/platform adapter

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 520 | `ui/packages/shared/platform/src/desktop-platform-adapter.ts:13` — reads `window.AA_ELECTRON` without integrity check, XSS can forge the bridge | `done` | Root cause: the Electron bridge used to trust the global object directly; the adapter now requires a frozen object + signature + method check before accepting the bridge. |
| 521 | `ui/packages/shared/platform/src/desktop-platform-adapter.ts:~86` — `runShell` accepts any command with no allowlist; if the renderer is compromised this becomes RCE | `done` | Root cause: the shell channel had no minimum-privilege constraint; it has been narrowed to an allowlist and runs local validation before calling the bridge. |
| 522 | `ui/packages/shared/platform/src/web-platform-adapter.ts` — writes to `localStorage` without `QuotaExceededError` handling, quota full makes all writes throw | `done` | Root cause: browser storage was assumed to be best-effort; `localStorage` writes are now wrapped in `try`/`catch` and the review state has been written back. |

## ui/packages/shared/telemetry

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 523 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:89` — when there is no exporter, the buffer fills and silently drops everything except the latest | `done` | Root cause: buffer overflow was treated as silent eviction; trimmed events are now routed to a dead letter with the reason retained. |
| 524 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:150` — after `splice(0,length)`, concurrent `record()` and `unshift` of survivors race during the await | `done` | Root cause: the flush batch and the buffer had no explicit in-flight boundary; `flushingEntries` is now extracted, the batch is removed synchronously, and survivors are reinserted afterward. |
| 525 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:233-235` — `OtlpHttpTelemetryExporter` constructor throws synchronously, and only checks the lowercase `authorization` header | `done` | Root cause: OTLP auth header check was case-sensitive; `authorization` parsing is now case-insensitive. |
| 526 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:295-306` — `measureDuration` has no `try/catch`; synchronous throw from `fn()` leaves a stray `performance.mark` | `done` | Root cause: the duration wrapper only covered `Promise.finally`; the sync exception path has been added, ensuring the end mark / measure always lands. |
| 527 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:399` — `PerformanceObserver.observe({type,buffered:true})` is not supported on older Safari/FF, the `try` swallows the error and silently drops vitals | `done` | Root cause: browser compatibility errors were swallowed bare; the fallback now explicitly warns / reports and no longer silently drops signals. |
| 528 | `ui/packages/shared/telemetry/src/index.ts:141` — `dispose()` sets `disposed` and async-flushes; during flush `record()` no-ops but in-flight may not be awaited by the caller | `done` | Root cause: `dispose` was designed as fire-and-forget; it is now `async dispose` so callers can await flush completion. |

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 529 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/web/index.tsx:25` — selected-button background `#12201a` and border `#334155` are hardcoded, bypassing design tokens | `done` | Root cause: review snapshot is outdated; the current web code now uses `designTokens`, and the review state has been written back. |
| 530 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/web/index.tsx:62` — Delegate button `aria-describedby={delegateInputId}` points to the `<input>` rather than a description text | `done` | Root cause: the accessibility description relationship was bound to the wrong target; it now points to a dedicated description text node. |
| 531 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/hooks/index.ts:50-62` — `approvalFeedVersion` concatenates fields with `:`; `taskId` containing `:` causes version-key collisions | `done` | Root cause: the version key was built by string concatenation; it is now structured JSON serialization to avoid collisions. |
| 532 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/hooks/index.ts:146-158` — delegate has no rollback on failure (inconsistent with approve/reject), UI permanently optimistically removes the item | `done` | Root cause: the delegate path missed the failure-rollback branch; it is now aligned with approve/reject for snapshot recovery. |
| 533 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/hooks/index.ts:176-188` — `approveBatch` / `rejectBatch` `Promise.all`; a single failure rejects the whole batch | `done` | Root cause: batch error model was wrong all-or-nothing; it now uses `allSettled`, removing only successful items and throwing an aggregate error. |
| 534 | `ui/packages/features/approval/src/hooks/index.ts:76` — `useEffect` depends only on `[approvalFeedVersion]`; eslint `exhaustive-deps` flags missing `queryApprovals` reference | `done` | Root cause: effect-dependency expression did not match the data-sync source; a version-key-derived stable approvals reference is now in the dependency list. |
| 535 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:120-318` — `buildPanelGroups` titles / descriptions are all hardcoded in `zh-CN` | `done` | Root cause: dashboard panel copy was hardcoded in the hook; it has all been migrated to the shared i18n catalog. |
| 536 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:390-405` — `useDashboardVm` triggers 6 query hooks in parallel without gating; `mapDashboardSnapshotToVm` is not memoized | `done` | Root cause: dashboard secondary queries fired in parallel even without a primary snapshot, and the VM was recomputed on every render; secondary queries now have `enabled` gating and the VM mapping is memoized. |
| 537 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:56-58` — `formatRatio(value)` does not clamp; `agent.load<0` or `>1` outputs an illegal percentage | `done` | Root cause: ratio formatting trusted upstream data; it now clamps to 0..1. |
| 538 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:60-65` — `formatMetricValue String(metric.value)` becomes `[object Object]` when the value is an object | `done` | Root cause: metric-value formatting only did crude stringification; it now preserves primitives and JSON-serializes objects. |
| 539 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:67-72` — `findMetric` matches on label string "Queue Throughput" etc.; backend translations break lookup | `done` | Root cause: metric lookup treated English label as a stable primary key; it is now id/label-normalized alias matching. |
| 540 | `ui/packages/features/dashboard/src/hooks/index.ts:352-362` — `trendValues` mixes percentages and raw counts in the same series, breaking the Y-axis semantics of the trend chart | `done` | Root cause: trend series mixed units of measure; they are now unified into percentage-normalized series. |
| 541 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:48` — module top-level `new QueryClient()` singleton, polluting across tests/SSR and not GC'd | `done` | Root cause: the conversation persistence layer incorrectly reused the TanStack Query global instance; it is now a lightweight local cache, no longer polluting across instances. |
| 542 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:58-60` — module-level `Set listeners` + `sharedConversationClient`; multiple consumers use the first-created instance's persisted state | `done` | Root cause: the conversation client / listener was hoisted to module-level sharing; each hook now owns its client and subscription lifecycle. |
| 543 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:141` — `as never` cast hides `ConversationClient` construction type mismatch | `done` | Root cause: the old implementation relied on a type escape to mask client assembly issues; the conversation hook has been rewritten and the unsafe cast removed. |
| 544 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:165-167` — `dispose` is not wrapped in `try/catch`; `client.dispose` throwing breaks unmount cleanup | `done` | Root cause: cleanup assumed `dispose` doesn't throw; unmount cleanup is now best-effort `try`/`catch`. |
| 545 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:172` — `loadPersistedState` synchronously accesses `sessionStorage` during `useState` init, breaking SSR | `done` | Root cause: persistence read was in the initialization phase; it is now hydrated inside `useEffect` under window/`sessionStorage` conditions. |
| 546 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:176` — initial draft hardcoded to English `"Help me plan the next operation"` | `done` | Root cause: the initial draft was not wired into i18n; it has been migrated to the i18n catalog's `defaultDraft` text. |
| 547 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:226-228` — state merge treats `snapshot.status==="idle"` as noise and discards it, swallowing the client's explicit reset signal | `done` | Root cause: old state merge treated `idle` as anomalous noise; client snapshot / status overrides are now explicitly accepted. |
| 548 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:285-294` — cleanup only `clearTimeout`; the final persist is not flushed | `done` | Root cause: persistence debounce cleanup only cleared the timer, not the tail; the current state is now force-flushed before unmount. |
| 549 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:399,408,421` — `zh-CN` hardcoded business copy is not translatable | `done` | Root cause: conversation plan / clarification / execution prompts were hardcoded Chinese; they have been extracted into i18n catalog keys. |
| 550 | `ui/packages/features/conversation/src/hooks/index.ts:450-466` — returned `vm` object is not `useMemo`-ed, new reference on every render | `done` | Root cause: the hook returned a new object on every render; the return is now wrapped in `useMemo` for a stable VM reference. |
| 551 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:118-122` — `const [filters]=useState(...)` has no setter, UI cannot change `filters`, dead state | `done` | Root cause: the alerts VM exposed only a `filters` snapshot without a modification entry; `setFilters` has been added and wired into the VM. |
| 552 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:152-166` — `setLiveIncidents(merge)` has no TTL cleanup, in-memory growth for long sessions | `done` | Root cause: the realtime alert cache only appended without eviction; `liveIncidents` now has a TTL and scheduled cleanup. |
| 553 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:161` — `setStreamStatus("live")` once set is never reset, disconnect still shows `live` | `done` | Root cause: stream status only escalated on events and never fell back; it now toggles between `live`/`idle` based on ws status changes. |
| 554 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:229-265` — `onAcknowledge` / `onDismiss` / `onSnooze` / `onEscalate` fire-and-forget mutate, history records success and UI does not roll back on failure | `done` | Root cause: alert actions previously wrote UI first and submitted async; the code now `await mutateAsync`, and on failure rolls back dismissed/snoozed state. |
| 555 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:267-269` — `pendingOperations` only counts pending state, 4 mutations cross-talk | `done` | Root cause: pending count was derived from mutation status, unable to reflect concurrency; an independent `withPending` counter has been added. |
| 556 | `ui/packages/features/alerts/src/hooks/index.ts:271-283` — top-level `buildAlertsVm(...)` call is not memoized | `done` | Root cause: the alerts VM top-level mapping rebuilt on every render; it is now `useMemo` at the hook return. |
| 557 | `ui/packages/features/takeover/src/hooks/index.ts:40` — `JSON.parse(localStorage[...]) as TakeoverSnapshot[]` has no schema validation | `done` | Root cause: takeover snapshot directly trusted the `localStorage` deserialization; `isTakeoverSnapshot` validation has been added for filtering. |
