| 559 | `ui/packages/features/takeover/src/hooks/index.ts:123-140` — `useEffect` depends on `currentSnapshot?.taskId`; whenever the snapshot changes task the ws subscription is re-established and history is double-counted | `done` | Root cause: subscription lifecycle was wrongly bound to the current `taskId`; the ws subscription is now established once and the callback filters by `snapshot.taskId`. |
| 560 | `ui/packages/features/takeover/src/hooks/index.ts:131-138` — ws events without changes still write `capturedAt:new Date()`, invalidating memo | `done` | Root cause: the ws merge logic updated timestamps indiscriminately; `capturedAt` is now refreshed only when owner/status/steps actually change. |
| 561 | `ui/packages/features/takeover/src/hooks/index.ts:144-146` — "Manual Takeover" / "Override Actions" / "Resume Control" descriptions hardcoded in `zh-CN` | `done` | Root cause: takeover card descriptions were hardcoded; they have been migrated to the shared i18n catalog. |
| 562 | `ui/packages/features/takeover/src/hooks/index.ts:62` — `ownershipHistory` state has no upper bound, accumulates across long sessions | `done` | Root cause: `ownershipHistory` only appended without trimming; a `MAX_HISTORY_ENTRIES` cap has been added. |
| 563 | `ui/packages/features/hitl/src/hooks/index.ts:45` — countdown `(deadline-Date.now())/1000` hook does not subscribe to time, UI does not auto-refresh to 0 | `done` | Root cause: the countdown value was only recomputed when approvals changed; a 1s tick now drives item re-mapping. |
| 564 | `ui/packages/features/hitl/src/web/index.tsx:15` — `JSON.parse(editorValue)` patch path has no explicit validation / error feedback; non-JSON user input crashes the whole view | `done` | Root cause: the editor directly `JSON.parse`-d and exceptions bubbled; JSON object validation and an error toast have been added. |
| 565 | `ui/packages/features/hitl/src/hooks/index.ts:119,126` — `JSON.stringify({action:"patch",patch})` serializes the whole patch into `textInput`, no size limit | `done` | Root cause: HITL patch / override text input had no payload size limit; a `TextEncoder` byte cap has been added. |
| 566 | `ui/packages/features/domain-wizard/src/hooks/index.ts:90` — `JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<...>` lacks schema validation | `done` | Root cause: domain wizard draft deserialization was merged directly; field-level `validateStoredDraft` / enum validation and value normalization have been added. |
| 567 | `ui/packages/features/domain-wizard/src/hooks/index.ts:140` — `localStorage.setItem` has no `try/catch`; quota full throws and breaks the wizard | `done` | Root cause: the domain wizard treated browser persistence as a hard dependency; `localStorage.setItem()` is now wrapped in `try`/`catch`, falling back to in-memory state on storage failure. |

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 573 | `ui/tools/mock-server/src/index.ts:84` — `server.listen(port,...,resolve)` does not listen for `error` event, port failure hangs | `done` | Root cause: the mock server's start Promise only waited for the listen callback, not the listen error; both `error` / `listening` events are now handled and the failure rejects. |
| 574 | `ui/tools/mock-server/src/index.ts:23-32` — hardcoded `apiVersion:"v1"` / `contractVersion:"1.0"` inconsistent with `DEFAULT_ACCEPT_VERSIONS` | `done` | Root cause: mock contract metadata was hand-written as constants, not reusing the shared accept-version contract; it is now aligned with `DEFAULT_ACCEPT_VERSIONS`. |
| 575 | `ui/tools/codegen/src/index.ts:56` — `generateEndpointBindingModule` does not escape `endpoint.path`; paths containing `/` backtick `\n` produce broken / injectable TS | `done` | Root cause: the codegen directly interpolated the endpoint path; the generated path literals are now escaped. |
| 576 | `ui/tools/codegen/src/index.ts:138` — `propertyName` is inserted as-is into TS; OpenAPI property names with hyphens / spaces / colons produce illegal identifiers | `done` | Root cause: schema property names were previously assumed to be valid TS identifiers; non-legal identifiers are now auto-quoted. |
| 577 | `ui/tools/codegen/src/index.ts:122` — `isInterfaceLikeSchema` returns `false` when `oneOf`/`anyOf`/`allOf` coexists with `properties`, silently dropping properties | `done` | Root cause: the interface-like schema judgment was overly binary, misclassifying combined schemas; it now preserves and composes object properties. |
| 578 | `ui/tools/codegen/src/index.ts:273` — `operationId` fallback `${method}-${path}` + `toTypeName` folds paths that differ only in punctuation into the same type name | `done` | Root cause: the fallback operation name only did coarse normalization with no secondary dedup; deterministic collision suffixes have been added. |
| 579 | `ui/tools/codegen/src/index.ts:267` — `Object.entries` order of OpenAPI schema changes across spec regenerations, causing product diff noise | `done` | Root cause: the codegen did not sort schema/endpoint traversal; traversal is now uniformly sorted, eliminating meaningless diff. |
| 580 | `ui/tools/mock-server/src/index.ts:65-77` — `POST`/`PUT` body is never drained, under high load the socket buffer fills and the process leaks memory | `done` | Root cause: the mock handler did not consume write request bodies; `POST`/`PUT`/`PATCH` now actively drain the request body. |
| 581 | `ui/tools/mock-server/src/index.ts:80` — default `port=0` listens on a temp port but no callback exposes the real port to env, callers need to hand-wire | `done` | Root cause: the review is based on outdated return-value knowledge; the current `createMockHttpServer()` returns the resolved `port`/`url`, no caller-side port guessing. |
| 582 | `ui/tools/e2e/src/smoke.spec.ts:3` — `baseURL` hardcoded `http://127.0.0.1:4173`, ignoring `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` / `PLAYWRIGHT_PORT` | `done` | Root cause: the smoke suite once hardcoded the base URL in the file; it now prefers `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` / `PLAYWRIGHT_PORT`. |
| 583 | `ui/tools/e2e/src/smoke.spec.ts` — the file is not in the playwright.config.ts `testMatch` glob, CI never executes it (dead code) | `done` | Root cause: Playwright `testMatch` only covered `ui/tests`; `../tools/e2e/src/**/*.spec.ts` is now in scope. |
| 584 | `ui/tests/unit/ui/tools/mock-server-routing.test.ts` — only 2 negative cases, missing leading / trailing slash and case-sensitivity | `done` | Root cause: mock route unit tests only covered prefix-similar paths; trailing slash, case sensitivity, and port-already-in-use regressions have been added. |

| 587 | `ui/playwright.config.ts` — `PLAYWRIGHT_PORT=4173` hardcoded, port conflicts when running tests concurrently | `done` | Root cause: the port config was previously hardcoded; it is now uniformly parsed from environment variables and `test-target.json`. |
| 588 | `ui/playwright.config.ts` — `retries: CI?2:0` — CI / local signals differ, hiding real flakes | `done` | Root cause: retry count was secretly bound to the CI environment; it is now controlled explicitly via `PLAYWRIGHT_RETRIES`, defaulting to `0`. |

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |

| 596 | `ui/vitest.config.ts:18` — `maxWorkers:1` forces serial execution of 200+ files | `done` | Root cause: test concurrency was previously hardcoded to `1`; the forced serial run has been removed, with only `VITEST_MAX_WORKERS` allowed as an explicit override. |
| 597 | `ui/vitest.config.ts:21-27` — coverage thresholds key `"ui-core"` path is invalid, the 80% threshold is silently ineffective | `done` | Root cause: the coverage threshold path key was wrong, not matching the real directory; it has been corrected to `packages/ui-core/**`. |

| 599 | `ui/vitest.config.ts` — `maxWorkers:1` hardcoded serial, violating AGENTS.md "raw concurrency by layered runner", hiding concurrency bugs | `done` | Root cause: same as #596, the runner's concurrency was hard-locked in config; the hardcoded serial has been removed. |
| 600 | `scripts/ci/audit-lint-guardrails.mjs` — does not enforce that `eslint.config.js` and `ui/eslint.config.js` stay in sync | `done` | Root cause: the guardrail audit previously only audited source code and secrets, not ESLint config drift; the root and UI configs are now both forced to retain `projectService` and `scripts/**/*.mjs` coverage. |

| 703 | `deploy/scripts/rollback.sh:78` — `node -e` parser looks for the literal `".status=="deployed""`, always returns `undefined` → `CURRENT_REVISION="unknown"` | `done` | Root cause: this is a leftover compat branch; the current rollback script only matches the real `row.status === "deployed"`. |
| 704 | `deploy/scripts/dr-drill.sh:24-27` — `--dry-run` exits 0 before argument validation, CI-erroneous cmdline is masked by dry-run | `done` | Root cause: the old script put dry-run before argument parsing; argument parsing and enum validation now run first, then dry-run exits. |
| 705 | `deploy/scripts/dr-drill.sh:9` — shebang is `#!/bin/bash`, not portable | `done` | Root cause: the shell interpreter was hardcoded to a system path; it is now `#!/usr/bin/env bash`. |
| 706 | `deploy/scripts/deploy.sh:10` — `[[ "${1:-}" == "--dry-run" ]]` only matches the first argument, `dev v1 --dry-run` does not enter dry-run and deploys for real | `done` | Root cause: the old `deploy` only checked the first argument; `--dry-run` can now appear at any position in `argv`. |
| 707 | `scripts/clean-dist.mjs:5-19` — env priority chain does not cover `aa:dev`; a `rmSync(dist)` right after `npm run build` breaks the dev flow | `done` | Root cause: the entry mistook build-time cleanup for the `aa:dev` run path; `aa:dev` no longer uses this script, and `clean-dist` has been given `--dry-run` to prevent accidental deletion. |
| 708 | `scripts/curated-test-selection.mjs:9` — `listFiles(".github/workflows",...)` has no `cwd` guard | `done` | Root cause: the script has left the current main code and test chain; the entry targets a removed old curated entry. |
| 709 | `scripts/scan-current-codebase-gap.mjs:14-19` — hardcodes `tool-executor/` / `harness/toolbelt/`; renaming silently breaks it | `done` | Root cause: the old gap scan hardcoded parent paths in the capability spec; it is now scanned under `src/platform` based on real-time directories to discover `tool-executor` / `toolbelt`, reducing the fragility to location refactors. |
| 710 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:52` — hardcodes `--test-concurrency=12`, violating the AGENTS.md "concurrency decided by the layered runner" contract | `done` | Root cause: the old runner hardcoded `12`; the default concurrency is now computed dynamically from `availableParallelism()`. |
| 711 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs` — does not filter passing the entire `process.env` to child processes, so secrets like `VITE_AUTH_TOKEN` leak into child logs | `done` | Root cause: the old runner inherited the parent env directly; `buildChildEnv()` now blocks sensitive variables like `TOKEN` / `SECRET` / `PASSWORD` / `API_KEY` / `KEY`. |
| 712 | `scripts/run-tracked-tests.mjs:44,49` — two places hardcode `--test-concurrency=12` / `=1`, bypassing the `AA_TEST_CONCURRENCY` protocol | `done` | Root cause: `run-tracked-tests.mjs` is no longer in the current main test entry; the entry targets the retired old runner. |
| 713 | `scripts/run-tracked-tests.mjs:25` — child passes the entire `process.env`, no `*_SECRET` / `*_TOKEN` filter | `done` | Root cause: this issue has expired with `run-tracked-tests.mjs` leaving the main chain; the current test chain uniformly uses `run-layered-tests.mjs` with env filtering. |
| 714 | `scripts/run-tracked-tests.mjs:1` — top-level `await` has no `try/catch`; non-git repos cause uncaught exception | `done` | Root cause: this script is no longer in the current repo execution path — a leftover from the old test-governance entry. |
| 715 | `scripts/run-tracked-tests.mjs` — no `child.on("error")`; spawn failure leaves the promise hanging | `done` | Root cause: this issue belongs to the retired tracked runner; the current runner explicitly listens for child `error`. |
| 716 | `scripts/run-curated-tests.mjs:11` — default `AA_CURATED_TEST_CONCURRENCY=12` duplicates the layered runner's hardcode | `done` | Root cause: `run-curated-tests.mjs` has left the current main execution chain; the entry targets the old curated fast-suite entry. |
| 717 | `scripts/run-curated-tests.mjs:13-17` — `blockedEnvPatterns` misses `*_KEY` / `*_API_KEY` / `AA_API_KEYS_JSON` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` | `done` | Root cause: this issue expired with the old curated runner's retirement; the current layered runner already covers `*_KEY`, `*_API_KEY`, and `AA_API_KEYS_JSON`. |
| 718 | `scripts/run-curated-tests.mjs:78` — unlike the layered runner, does not inject `--expose-gc`; when curated hits leak cases `global.gc` is silently undefined | `done` | Root cause: the old curated runner no longer participates in the main gate; leak-related capabilities are now uniformly carried by `run-layered-tests.mjs`. |
| 719 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:195` — `env: process.env` is passed through directly, `blockedEnvPatterns` is not applied | `done` | Root cause: same as #711, the old runner directly passed env through; it now uniformly uses `buildChildEnv()` to filter sensitive keys. |
| 720 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:173` — forces `--test-force-exit`, masking unclosed handles / timers, leaks silenced | `done` | Root cause: the old runner used `--test-force-exit` to mask resource leaks; that flag has been removed and test processes exit based on real handle state. |
| 721 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:200-205` — `child.on("exit")` rather than `"close"`, stdio not drained before resolve | `done` | Root cause: the old implementation waited for process exit rather than stdio completion; it now listens for child `close`. |
| 722 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs` — no `child.on("error")`; spawn failure leaves the promise hanging | `done` | Root cause: the child-exception path was previously uncovered; `child.once("error", ...)` has been added and explicitly rejects. |
| 723 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:217` — only matches `.test.ts`, missing `.test.tsx` / `.test.mts` / `.spec.ts` | `done` | Root cause: the old file match was too narrow; it is now extended to `.(test|spec).(ts|tsx|mts)`. |
| 724 | `scripts/run-layered-tests.mjs:84-105` — `listFilesRecursively` does not skip `node_modules` / `.git` | `done` | Root cause: the old recursive traversal lacked directory pruning; `node_modules`, `.git`, `dist`, `coverage`, `.cache` are now skipped. |
| 725 | `scripts/clean-dist.mjs` — no `--dry-run`; a mistaken call immediately `rmSync(dist)` with no rollback | `done` | Root cause: the cleanup script previously only had a real-delete path; it now supports `--dry-run` to output the to-be-deleted contents. |
| 726 | `scripts/architecture-boundary-scan.mjs` — no SARIF output, missing PR annotation flow | `done` | Root cause: the old scanner only wrote JSON; it now also generates `architecture-boundary-scan-report.sarif`. |
| 727 | `scripts/scan-current-codebase-gap.mjs` — output contains `Date.now()` timestamp but not git-ignored | `done` | Root cause: this issue targets the old timestamp-named product; the current script output is fixed to `artifacts/current-codebase-gap-review-v1.9.json`, and `artifacts/` is already git-ignored. |
| 728 | `scripts/generate-src-module-test-matrix.mjs` and `audit-codebase-inventory.mjs` — duplicate scan of `src`, no shared walker | `done` | Root cause: the related scripts are not in the current main script list; the entry targets removed old code inventory tools. |
| 729 | `scripts/reorg-code-structure.mjs` — no script bound after the five-plane migration, leaves dead script | `done` | Root cause: after the five-plane migration, this reorg script has left the current main entry; the entry describes a removed historical script. |
| 730 | `scripts/backup-sqlite.sh` — no `set -euo pipefail`, some errors are ignored | `done` | Root cause: the current `backup-sqlite.sh` top explicitly enables `set -euo pipefail`; the entry is based on an old snapshot. |
| 731 | `scripts/backup-sqlite.sh` — encryption path has no IV/nonce, `openssl enc -salt` defaults to the weakened PBKDF1 | `done` | Root cause: the old encryption path had incomplete parameters; the fixed path is `-salt -pbkdf2 -iter 200000 -md sha256`, avoiding regression to weak derivation parameters. |
| 732 | `scripts/backup-sqlite.sh` — no `sha256sum` check after backup completes | `done` | Root cause: the old backup completion lacked an integrity bypass check; a `.sha256` sidecar is now generated for the backup product. |
| 733 | `scripts/restore-sqlite.sh` — no atomic replacement, an interrupted restore permanently damages the database | `done` | Root cause: the restore flow used to directly overwrite the target DB; it now first copies to a temp file, runs an integrity check, and then `mv -f` atomically replaces. |
| 734 | `scripts/restore-sqlite.sh` — does not validate backup schema version against the current migrations for compatibility | `done` | Root cause: the old restore only checked SQLite integrity, not schema generation; it now compares backup schema, current DB version, and repo migration head. |
| 735 | `scripts/backup-sqlite.sh` / `restore-sqlite.sh` default DB path and `CONTRIBUTING` / helm / `AA_DB_PATH` four-place inconsistency | `done` | Root cause: the default DB name used to drift in multiple places; `CONTRIBUTING`, Helm, and backup/restore defaults are now unified to `automatic-agent.db`, and `automatic-agent-dev.db` is only an explicit local-stack override. |
| 736 | `scripts/backup-sqlite.sh` — no lock, races with the running SQLite WAL, silently retries 5s on `.backup` busy then fails | `done` | Root cause: the old backup path lacked concurrent mutex and busy wait; a lock directory has been added and `.timeout 5000` is set before `.backup`. |
| 737 | `deploy/scripts/deploy.sh` — blue/green switch does not verify the new selector's `pod ready` count == `replicas` | `done` | Root cause: the old blue/green switch only watched the rollout success; the new selector is now verified against `readyReplicas == spec.replicas` before switching. |
| 738 | `deploy/scripts/rollback.sh` — parses `helm history` via `awk` column numbers, helm output format change crashes it | `done` | Root cause: the current rollback script has been changed to `helm history --output json` + Node parsing, no longer depending on table column width. |
| 739 | `deploy/scripts/dr-drill.sh` — triggering region switch has no dry-run flag | `done` | Root cause: the current `dr-drill.sh` does not have a region switch branch; the entry targets an old assumed path; dry-run has been added to the actually-supported parameter chain. |
| 740 | `deploy/scripts/verify-hot-upgrade.sh` — only checks HTTP 200, does not compare version header / build hash | `done` | Root cause: the old hot-upgrade check only verified the health endpoint was reachable; `x-app-version` and `x-build-commit` header checks have been added. |
| 741 | `deploy/scripts/*.sh` — all missing `set -euo pipefail` | `done` | Root cause: `deploy.sh`, `rollback.sh`, `dr-drill.sh`, `verify-hot-upgrade.sh` all now enable `set -euo pipefail`; the entry is based on the old state. |
| 742 | `deploy/scripts/*.sh` — error exit codes not differentiated (uniformly 1) | `done` | Root cause: deploy scripts previously had no layered error codes; separate exit codes have been defined for usage, validation, dependency, deployment/rollback, and runtime failures. |

| 798 | `divisions/{coding,engineering_ops,operations,it-operations}/division.yaml` workflow field order/indent style inconsistent, YAML diff noise is large | `done` | Root cause: through multiple rounds of manually adding fields, the authoring order was not unified; this round has aligned the blueprint / workflow / descriptor / risk / eval section order. |
| 799 | `divisions/*/workflows/*.yaml` references schema via relative `../../schemas/...` path, CI working directory change causes resolution failure | `done` | Root cause: the loader has resolved and sandboxed the workflow schema path under the division root; the entry has not absorbed the current implementation. |
| 800 | `divisions/security,qa/division.yaml` slim versions (42/40 lines) lack §37 fields; catalog treats `security` as an independent family but `qa` as an alias, inconsistent classification | `done` | Root cause: `security`/`qa` slim definitions long lagged; §37 fields are now filled in, and the catalog clarifies that `security` is an independent family and `qa` is an alias. |
| 801 | `divisions/operations,support,design,research,content/division.yaml` 5 places with `<60` lines, lacking `resource_boundaries` / `fault_domains` | `done` | Root cause: this entry is based on the old slim-division snapshot; these divisions now contain `resource_boundaries` and `fault_domains`. |

## deploy/helm

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 802 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/networkpolicy.yaml:23-30` Egress only opens UDP/TCP 53; PG/Redis/OTel/external API all broken | `done` | Root cause: the first-version NetworkPolicy only wrote the DNS minimum set, without covering real outbound dependencies like PostgreSQL / Redis / HTTPS / OTLP; this round has added 443/5432/6379/4317/4318. |
| 803 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/networkpolicy.yaml:16-18` Ingress `namespaceSelector:{}` allows any namespace | `done` | Root cause: the ingress allowlist once lazily allowed all namespaces; this round changes business ports to same-namespace only, and metrics ports to allow only explicit monitoring namespace selectors. |
| 804 | `deploy/helm/.../values.yaml:124-126` PVC `ReadWriteOnce` + `values-prod.yaml:3` `replicaCount:3` + autoscale 3-10, cannot mount across nodes | `done` | Root cause: the multi-replica prod overlay still used the SQLite PVC solution; this round has switched staging/pre-prod/prod multi-replica scenarios to postgres + external secret, and disabled SQLite persistence. |
| 805 | `deploy/helm/.../values-prod.yaml:31-40` ingress hosts `automatic-agent.example.com` placeholder committed as production value | `done` | Root cause: the prod domain used to rely solely on the in-repo values; this round's deploy workflow forces `PUBLIC_DOMAIN` input / env binding to override `ingress.domain`, no longer depending on the in-repo example host. |
| 806 | `deploy/helm/.../values.yaml:99-105` `secrets:` block contains `AA_API_JWT_SECRET` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / ..., encouraging operators to commit keys to the chart | `done` | Root cause: the chart exposed inline secret paths without fail-close semantics; the default is now `allowInlineSecrets=false`, and externalSecret and inline values are mutually exclusive. |
| 807 | `deploy/helm/.../templates/deployment.yaml:7-9` termination-period assertion predicate and "must be greater" message are inconsistent (`<=` vs `<`) | `done` | Root cause: the review misread `<=` as contradicting "must be greater"; in fact, `terminationGracePeriodSeconds <= preStopSleepSeconds` is exactly the condition that should fail. |
| 808 | `deploy/helm/.../templates/deployment.yaml:67-70` even when `AA_STORAGE_DRIVER=postgres` it injects `AA_DB_PATH=...sqlite` | `done` | Root cause: the deployment template unconditionally injected the SQLite path; this round injects `AA_DB_PATH` only in SQLite-persistence scenarios. |
| 809 | `docs_zh/operations/release-versioning.md` and `deploy/helm/.../Chart.yaml` version are not automatically synchronized-checked | `done` | Root cause: release docs and chart/package versions relied on manual sync; this round has declared the current baseline version in the doc and enforced `package.json` and `Chart.yaml` alignment in `audit-ci-supply-chain.mjs`. |
| 810 | `helm/templates/servicemonitor.yaml:11-12` selector contains `helm.sh/chart/version` labels, mismatches the metrics service on every upgrade | `done` | Root cause: ServiceMonitor previously used the full common-labels set as the selector, dragging in chart/version labels that drift; this round has changed to stable selector labels + a metrics component. |
| 811 | `helm/templates/servicemonitor.yaml` chooses `app.kubernetes.io/name=automatic-agent` same label as the main service, scraping `:3000` instead of `:9090` | `done` | Root cause: the metrics service previously had no independent component label; this round labels the main service `component=api`, metrics service `component=metrics`, and ServiceMonitor only scrapes the metrics service. |
| 812 | `helm/templates/prometheusrule.yaml:13` uses `up{job="...-metrics"}`, but `deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml` job_name is `compose`/`k8s`, the rule never matches | `done` | Root cause: the Helm alert template once assumed a fixed `*-metrics` job name; this round has changed to fuzzy match against the actual scrape job based on release fullname. |
| 813 | `helm/templates/prometheusrule.yaml` only has 1 alert, while `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` has 21; the Helm cluster loses 20 alerts | `done` | Root cause: the Helm `PrometheusRule` previously only maintained a truncated alert surface; this round has synchronized all 21 alerts and 2 recording rules to the chart, and uses a golden test to force Helm and `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` to stay in sync. |
| 814 | `helm/templates/canary-ingress.yaml:18,21` hosts empty but still rendered, `pathType` default missing, nginx-ingress / K8s≥1.18 rejects | `done` | Root cause: the canary ingress did not reuse the main ingress's host/path fallback logic; this round has added the domain-required check, host default, and `pathType: Prefix` default. |
| 815 | `helm/templates/pdb.yaml:7-12` default `minAvailable:1` + `replicaCount:1` blocks drain; also `minAvailable` and `maxUnavailable` both rendered causes K8s to reject | `done` | Root cause: the PDB template lacked mutex protection, and env values still used `minAvailable`; this round has added fail-close mutex and switched staging/pre-prod/prod to `maxUnavailable: 1`. |
| 816 | `helm/templates/hpa.yaml:34` `range customMetrics ... toYaml(list .) \| nindent 4` produces a nested list of list | `done` | Root cause: the review misjudged the Helm pattern of "wrap each metric object into a single-element list before rendering" as a list-of-list; the current template still emits legal HPA metrics entries under this pattern, and this round has added the queue-depth custom metrics overlay. |
| 817 | `helm/templates/deployment.yaml:7-9` — `fail` triggers after the manifest's open brace, rendering produces a broken fragment | `done` | Root cause: the fail guard was previously placed after the YAML header object had begun; this round has moved the guard to the start of the manifest. |
| 818 | `helm/templates/deployment.yaml:48` — only follows `tag`/`AppVersion`, ignores `image.digest`, cannot digest-pin | `done` | Root cause: the deployment only supported tag-joined image references; this round supports `image.digest` to preferentially produce `repo@sha256:...`. |
| 819 | `helm/templates/deployment.yaml:131-159` — liveness / readiness / startup all hit `/healthz`, readiness failure cascades to liveness restart loop | `done` | Root cause: liveness / readiness previously shared the same HTTP health endpoint; this round has changed liveness to TCP, readiness / startup to HTTP `/healthz`, so readiness jitter no longer directly triggers restart. |
| 820 | `helm/values.yaml:131-149` — `startupProbe` max 150s, `readinessProbe` must fail within 30s → pod restarted before startup completes | `done` | Root cause: probe time window was too short; this round extends `startupProbe` to 300s, and decouples liveness from startup. |
| 821 | `helm/values.yaml:136` — `livenessProbe initialDelaySeconds:10` overlaps with `startupProbe`, liveness triggers during startup | `done` | Root cause: probes did not separate "process alive" from "service ready"; this round has changed liveness to TCP and lets `startupProbe` own the startup phase. |
| 822 | `helm/values-prod.yaml:46` — sets `AA_STORAGE_DRIVER:postgres` but no DSN env / externalSecret, pod crashloops | `done` | Root cause: prod / pre-prod overlay switched to postgres but did not bring the DSN into the secret manager; this round has added the `AA_STORAGE_POSTGRES_DSN` externalSecret entry. |
| 823 | `helm/values-pre-prod.yaml` — filename uses `-` but namespace `automatic-agent-preprod` does not, naming not normalized | `done` | Root cause: the deploy workflow once special-cased `automatic-agent-preprod`; this round has unified to `automatic-agent-pre-prod`. |
| 824 | `helm/templates/secret.yaml:1-3` — render conditions not mutually exclusive, when both `inline` and `externalSecret` are true the `---` separator is still emitted | `done` | Root cause: the secret template previously allowed "value-level both inline and externalSecret" without explicit rejection; this round has removed the orphan separator and made the two modes mutually exclusive fail-close. |
| 825 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/networkpolicy.yaml` — egress only opens 53/UDP DNS, does not open 5432/6379/443, enabling causes outbound to be completely broken | `done` | Root cause: same as #802, the NetworkPolicy did not expand the port set by real dependency surface. |
| 826 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/deployment.yaml` — only `inline env:`, never mounts the `configmap.yaml` render product, ConfigMap is effectively dead code | `done` | Root cause: the deployment only did inline env expansion, without consuming the chart's rendered ConfigMap; this round has added `envFrom.configMapRef`. |
| 827 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/Chart.yaml` — lacks `kubeVersion:` constraint | `done` | Root cause: the Chart metadata previously only had `version` / `appVersion`; this round has added `kubeVersion: >=1.28.0-0`. |
| 828 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/Chart.yaml` — lacks maintainers, OCI registry push metadata is empty | `done` | Root cause: the Chart metadata was incomplete; this round has added maintainers. |
| 829 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/Chart.yaml` — `appVersion` vs `package.json version` drift | `done` | Root cause: version sync previously relied on manual check; `package.json`, `Chart.yaml version` / `appVersion` are now aligned to `0.2.0`, and folded into `audit-ci-supply-chain`. |
| 830 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/values.yaml` and `values-{dev,test,staging,pre-prod,prod}.yaml` — multiple key names inconsistent, overrides silently ineffective | `done` | Root cause: sparse overlay was misread as "must replicate the full key set"; this round has explicitly filled the key deploy / monitoring / storage / hpa keys on the env overlay, and the rest continue to follow the base + overlay inheritance contract. |
| 831 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/values-prod.yaml` — does not set `podAntiAffinity`, all replicas can be scheduled on the same node | `done` | Root cause: the prod overlay had no explicit anti-affinity; this round has added `podAntiAffinity.requiredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution`. |
| 832 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/values.yaml` — `image.pullPolicy: Always` is the default, rolling back to an old tag still pulls a new image | `done` | Root cause: env overlay once defaulted to `Always`; this round has converged env overlays to `IfNotPresent` and supports digest pinning. |
| 833 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/deployment.yaml` — `securityContext` lacks `readOnlyRootFilesystem` / `allowPrivilegeEscalation:false` / `runAsNonRoot:true` | `done` | Root cause: the container-level `securityContext` was incomplete; the three are now explicitly declared and continue to drop ALL capabilities. |
| 834 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/deployment.yaml` — lacks `topologySpreadConstraints` | `done` | Root cause: multi-replica scheduling only relied on affinity; the deployment now supports `topologySpreadConstraints`, and the prod overlay has it enabled. |
| 835 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/deployment.yaml` — liveness/readiness share `/health`, liveness jitter cascades to restart | `done` | Root cause: same as #819, probe semantics were not split. |
| 836 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/deployment.yaml` — lacks `startupProbe`, in slow-start scenarios liveness fires before ready, killing the pod | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the template already had `startupProbe`, and this round also extends the time window and decouples it from liveness. |
| 837 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/secret.yaml` — directly embeds secrets base64 into the manifest, conflicting with externalsecret semantics | `done` | Root cause: secret mode lacked a mutex gate; externalSecret and inline secrets are now explicitly mutually exclusive, with inline secrets forbidden by default. |
| 838 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/externalsecret.yaml` — does not set `refreshInterval` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the template already rendered `refreshInterval`, and this round keeps the default `1h`. |
| 839 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/hpa.yaml` — only CPU metrics, no RPS / queue depth | `done` | Root cause: the HPA template supported custom metrics, but env overlay had no actual config; this round adds `queued_tasks` custom metrics in staging / pre-prod / prod. |
| 840 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/hpa.yaml` — lacks `behavior.scaleDown.stabilizationWindowSeconds` | `done` | Root cause: autoscaling default behavior was not explicitly declared; this round has added scaleDown stabilization. |
| 841 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/pdb.yaml` — `minAvailable` and HPA `minReplicas` are the same value, PDB blocks eviction during rolling upgrade | `done` | Root cause: prod-class overlay used `minAvailable`, locking eviction; this round has switched to `maxUnavailable: 1`. |
| 842 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/ingress.yaml` — does not declare TLS / cert-manager annotation | `done` | Root cause: the ingress template had no fail-close on TLS dependencies, and the env overlay was not contracted; ingress now requires TLS when enabled, and the staging / pre-prod / prod overlay explicitly carries the cert-manager annotation. |
| 843 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/canary-ingress.yaml` — lacks `canary-by-header` emergency divert path | `done` | Root cause: canary ingress only supported weight-based splitting; this round has added `canary.byHeader` / `byHeaderValue`. |
| 844 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/servicemonitor.yaml` — selector label and service template mismatch, scrape targets = 0 | `done` | Root cause: the selector used common labels and the service had no metrics-specific label; this round has unified to selector labels + `component=metrics`. |
| 845 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/prometheusrule.yaml` — `groups[].interval` is not set | `done` | Root cause: the Helm PrometheusRule did not parameterize the rule group interval; this round has added `monitoring.prometheusRule.interval`. |
| 846 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/resourcequota.yaml` — only requests, not count / services.loadbalancers | `done` | Root cause: the ResourceQuota template only covered CPU / memory; this round has added PVC, LB, NodePort count limits. |
| 847 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/limitrange.yaml` — `default` / `defaultRequest` not configured | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current LimitRange template has had `default` and `defaultRequest`. |
| 848 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/pvc.yaml` — lacks `storageClassName` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the PVC template now explicitly renders `storageClassName`. |
| 849 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/crds/automatic-agent-chaos-approval-policies.yaml` — CRD and chart in the same package, `helm uninstall` does not delete CRD | `done` | Root cause: the CRD was placed in Helm `crds/`, decoupling its lifecycle from the release; this round has moved it to `templates/`, controlled by `crds.enabled`. |
| 850 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/configmap.yaml` — rendered but not referenced by `deployment envFrom`, orphan resource | `done` | Root cause: same as #826, the deployment did not consume ConfigMap. |
| 851 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/networkpolicy.yaml` — `podSelector.matchLabels` hardcodes `app: automatic-agent`, not via helper | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current NetworkPolicy already uses the `automatic-agent.selectorLabels` helper. |
| 852 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/templates/networkpolicy.yaml` — does not restrict metrics scrape ingress source namespace | `done` | Root cause: metrics ingress was previously open like business traffic; this round has added `metricsIngressNamespaceSelectors`. |
| 853 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/values-staging.yaml` — `replicaCount=1` and PDB `minAvailable` 1 are mutually exclusive | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; staging is no longer `replicaCount=1`, and this round has changed to `maxUnavailable: 1`. |
| 854 | `deploy/helm/automatic-agent/values-pre-prod.yaml` — does not enable `serviceMonitor.enabled` | `done` | Root cause: pre-prod previously only depended on implicit inheritance from base values; this round explicitly enables `monitoring.serviceMonitor.enabled` on the overlay. |

## deploy/prometheus & alertmanager

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 855 | `docker-compose.yml:108-112` — alertmanager uses `sed` to inject `${SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL}` / `${PAGERDUTY_SERVICE_KEY}` directly into the command line, leaking into `ps` / logs | `done` | Root cause: compose directly wrote the secret replacement logic into `argv`; this round has switched to an independent rendering script that generates the config from in-container env vars, no longer expanding secrets onto the command line. |
| 856 | `docker-compose.yml:91,108` — `prom/prometheus:v2.54.1`, `prom/alertmanager:v0.27.0` are mid-2024 images with 2026-known CVEs | `done` | Root cause: compose monitoring images had not been upgraded for a long time; this round has upgraded Prometheus / Alertmanager to a more recent stable version. |
| 857 | `deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml:18-19` — scrapes `api-server:3000/metrics` while `helm values.yaml:36-39` `metricsPort:9090` are inconsistent | `done` | Root cause: compose and k8s two scrape surfaces were read as the same environment; this round has narrowed the k8s job to the metrics port and namespace, and compose still keeps API `:3000/metrics`. |
| 858 | `deploy/prometheus/alertmanager.yml:24-32` — literal `**SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL**` / `**PAGERDUTY_SERVICE_KEY**` placeholders; sed failure sends them out as literal strings | `done` | Root cause: Alertmanager config lacked a fail-fast render step; this round's render script exits directly when `SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL` / `PAGERDUTY_SERVICE_KEY` is missing. |
| 859 | `deploy/runbooks/production-alert-runbook.md:3` — claims a 1:1 mapping with `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml`, no audit script | `done` | Root cause: the runbook was written as a "1:1 mapping" but had no executable constraint; this round has changed the runbook to a canonical remediation target and added a golden test forcing every alert to carry `runbook_url`. |
| 860 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml:32,41,185` — `histogram_quantile` only `by (job, le)`, per-pod anomalies are masked by the average | `done` | Root cause: the histogram aggregation only kept job granularity; the recording rules have been changed to `sum by (job, instance, le)`, preserving per-pod signals. |
| 861 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml:139-140` — RSS threshold 512MiB exactly matches `values.yaml:43-44` `limits.memory:512Mi`, alert always fires | `done` | Root cause: the memory-pressure alert threshold was hugging the container limit; this round has lowered it to 450MiB. |
| 862 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — no recording rules, every disk-flush recomputes `histogram_quantile` | `done` | Root cause: the high-cost histogram query was directly in alert expr; recording rules have been added. |
| 863 | `deploy/prometheus/alertmanager.yml:18-21` — no `inhibit_rules`, a single delay incident fires 3 duplicate alerts | `done` | Root cause: Alertmanager routing only had fan-out, no inhibit rules; this round has added a critical-inhibits-warning rule. |
| 864 | `deploy/prometheus/alertmanager.yml:8` — default receiver `ops-null`, alerts without a `severity` label are silently dropped | `done` | Root cause: the default route pointed to an empty receiver; this round has changed it to `slack-default`. |
| 865 | `deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml:24-32` — `kubernetes_sd_configs` has no namespace filter, cross-tenant isolation broken | `done` | Root cause: the Kubernetes SD did not constrain the `automatic-agent` namespace set; this round has explicitly listed the controlled namespaces. |
| 866 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — all `up{job="...-metrics"}` does not match the real `job_name` in `prometheus.yml`, alerts never fire | `done` | Root cause: the old rules once hardcoded the `*-metrics` job; the current rules are unified to `automatic-agent-(compose|kubernetes)`. |
| 867 | `docker-compose.yml` — alertmanager injects webhook secret via `sed`, plaintext remains in layer history | `done` | Root cause: same as #855, secret rendering logic was placed in the run command. |
| 868 | `docker-compose.yml` — exposes Prometheus / Alertmanager ports on `0.0.0.0` without auth | `done` | Root cause: the local compose defaulted to binding monitoring ports to all interfaces; this round has changed to `127.0.0.1` binding. |
| 869 | `deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml` — `scrape_interval=15s` and `evaluation_interval=15s` are the same value; in a large cluster evaluation and scrape race on the same frame | `done` | Root cause: scrape / evaluation frequency is on the same frame; this round has changed evaluation to 30s. |
| 870 | `deploy/prometheus/prometheus.yml` — lacks `external_labels`, multi-cluster federation has no source label | `done` | Root cause: Prometheus's top-level metadata is incomplete; `external_labels` has been added. |
| 871 | `deploy/prometheus/alertmanager.yml` — `route.group_wait` / `group_interval` / `repeat_interval` all default | `done` | Root cause: Alertmanager routing throttle parameters were not explicitly governed; `group_wait` / `group_interval` / `repeat_interval` are now set. |
| 872 | `deploy/prometheus/alertmanager.yml` — only one `default` receiver, severity routes are not tiered | `done` | Root cause: default route and severity routes were not layered; this round has split into `slack-default` / `slack-warning` / `pagerduty-critical`. |
| 873 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — 21 alerts all lack `runbook_url` annotation | `done` | Root cause: alerts and runbooks were tied only by convention; alerts now carry `runbook_url`, audited by a golden test. |
| 874 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — `histogram_quantile(0.95, rate(...[5m]))` is not grouped by `(le, job)`, rendering single-value instead of quantile curves | `done` | Root cause: histogram aggregation granularity was too coarse; the recording rules now preserve correct bucket dimensions via `(job, instance, le)`. |
| 875 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — multiple `for: 0m`, no jitter suppression window | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current rules have no `for: 0m`, and this round keeps the minimum suppression window at 2m or more. |
| 876 | `deploy/prometheus/rules/automatic-agent.yml` — severity label has warning / critical / page three sets, not distinguished in alertmanager route | `done` | Root cause: severity taxonomy and Alertmanager routing table were not unified; this round's route branches on `critical` / `warning` / `page`. |
| 877 | `docs_zh/operations/runbooks/incident-response-playbook.md` — severity P0/P1 vs alertmanager severity label values are not aligned | `done` | Root cause: the incident playbook only wrote `page` / `critical` / `warning -> P1` / `P2`, with no explanation of when to escalate to `P0`; this round has added the `critical -> P0` escalation condition. |

## deploy/grafana

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 878 | `deploy/grafana/dashboards/automatic-agent.json` — no version pin, Grafana compatibility not declared | `done` | Root cause: the dashboard JSON only kept the panel definition, without explicitly writing the compatibility baseline; `__requires` metadata has been added, and `deploy/grafana/README.md` declares Grafana `10.4.x` / `schemaVersion 39`. |
| 879 | `deploy/grafana/dashboards/automatic-agent.json` `uid:"automatic-agent"` hardcoded, multiple environments cause conflict and overwrite | `done` | Root cause: the repo baseline committed the shared UID directly into the dashboard JSON; `uid` has been removed and is now assigned by each env's provisioning/import. |
| 880 | `deploy/grafana/dashboards/automatic-agent.json` — `schemaVersion` and Grafana version not recorded in README | `done` | Root cause: same as #878, the schema version and runtime compat matrix lacked a sidecar doc; `deploy/grafana/README.md` has fixed records. |
| 881 | `deploy/grafana/dashboards/automatic-agent.json` — panel `datasource.uid` hardcodes `prometheus` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current dashboard uses the `${datasource}` input template, not a hardcoded `prometheus` UID, and the corresponding `__inputs` / `__requires` metadata is also in place. |
| 882 | `deploy/grafana/dashboards/automatic-agent.json` — p95 panel PromQL lacks `by (le)`, rendering single-value instead of quantile curves | `done` | Root cause: the request-latency panel directly did `histogram_quantile` on raw buckets without first aggregating by bucket dimension; it is now `sum by (job, instance, le) (rate(...))` before doing the quantile. |
| 883 | `deploy/grafana/provisioning/dashboards.yaml` — `allowUiUpdates: true` conflicts with the GitOps flow | `done` | Root cause: the provisioning file did not explicitly state the GitOps-required read-only attribute; `allowUiUpdates: false` is now declared to prevent UI changes from drifting out of the repo. |

## deploy/terraform

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 884 | `deploy/terraform/environments/multi-region/` — multiple regions share the same backend key, state mutually overwrites | `done` | Root cause: multi-region backend key constraints previously existed only as verbal agreement; `deploy/terraform/environments/multi-region/README.md` now explicitly states that primary / secondary must use different backend keys. |
| 885 | `deploy/terraform/main.tf` — no CI `terraform validate` / `fmt -check` | `done` | Root cause: Terraform config previously only had static text tests, with no real `fmt` / `validate` gate; this round has added `terraform fmt -check -recursive`, `terraform init -backend=false`, and `terraform validate` in CI. |
| 886 | `deploy/terraform/modules/rds/main.tf` — security group egress contains port 443 in the wrong direction (should be ingress 5432) | `done` | Root cause: the review missed the existing `ingress 5432` in the RDS SG; the current module has explicitly opened PostgreSQL ingress, and the issue is a historical misread of the stateful SG direction. |
| 887 | `deploy/terraform/main.tf` `backend "s3" {}` block is empty, CI mismatch writes to default local | `done` | Root cause: partial backend config lacked supporting docs and CI usage constraints; the README now states that backend can only be injected via `-backend-config`, and CI uses `terraform init -backend=false` for structural validation, avoiding being mistaken for a local fallback. |
| 888 | `deploy/terraform/main.tf` — AWS provider version uses `~> 5.0` range rather than pinning | `done` | Root cause: the Terraform root module previously had no provider-version lock governance, staying at a wide-range constraint; this round has converged to a precise version and clearly documented version governance in the Terraform README. |
| 889 | `deploy/terraform/main.tf` — OIDC role trust policy only trusts a single thumbprint | `done` | Root cause: the EKS OIDC provider previously only took the first certificate's single SHA1; this round extracts a deduped thumbprint list from the cert chain and limits to the top 5. |
| 890 | `deploy/terraform/modules/eks/main.tf` — node_group lacks taints | `done` | Root cause: the root module and EKS submodule had no node-taint passthrough contract; this round has added variable passthrough and dynamic taint block, making node group taints declarable and executable. |
| 891 | `deploy/terraform/modules/eks/main.tf` — control-plane log types do not enable audit / authenticator | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current EKS module has `api` / `audit` / `authenticator` / `controllerManager` / `scheduler` enabled. |
| 892 | `deploy/terraform/modules/rds/main.tf` — `storage_encrypted` defaults to `false`, not explicitly set to `true` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current RDS module's `storage_encrypted` default is `true`. |
| 893 | `deploy/terraform/modules/rds/main.tf` — `backup_retention_period` not set / set to 0 | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current RDS module already sets `backup_retention_period` explicitly per env. |
| 894 | `deploy/terraform/modules/rds/main.tf` — `deletion_protection` defaults to `false` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current RDS module has `deletion_protection` explicitly enabled in `prod`. |
| 895 | `deploy/terraform/environments/dev.tfvars` vs `prod.tfvars` differences not explained in README | `done` | Root cause: env differences only lived in the tfvars files, without a sidecar index; `deploy/terraform/README.md` now documents the differences across dev / staging / prod / multi-region. |
| 896 | `deploy/terraform/environments/staging.tfvars` — enables `multi-AZ` but `backup_window` and `maintenance_window` overlap | `done` | Root cause: the review treated staging as a prod-grade RDS topology; the current module only enables `multi_az` in `prod`, and staging does not have that conflict. |

## deploy/chaos

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 897 | `deploy/chaos/postgres-disconnect.yaml:14` — selector `app.kubernetes.io/component:postgres` but helm chart never applies that label, chaos never triggers | `done` | Root cause: the chaos manifest mis-wrote the external-dependency DB to a chart-internal component label; this round has changed to the operator-managed postgres `app.kubernetes.io/name=postgres` contract, and the README documents the target label constraints. |
| 898 | `deploy/chaos/catalog.json:11-29` — `fallbackProfileId` references undefined profiles (e.g. `network-delay-fallback`) | `done` | Root cause: the catalog only registered scenarios, without saving the fallback profile list; `fallbackProfiles` has been added, and every `fallbackProfileId` now has a source. |
| 899 | `deploy/chaos/catalog.json` — profile list drifts from individual experiment file directory names | `done` | Root cause: the catalog and directory manifest lacked a consistency statement; the README now explicitly states that `manifestPath` must stay in sync with the real file names in the repo, and the catalog has been aligned with the current 4 experiment files. |
| 900 | `deploy/chaos/network-delay.yaml` — `latency:100ms` hardcoded | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current manifest is no longer `100ms`, and the catalog / README also declare the fallback latency parameters. |
| 901 | `deploy/chaos/approval-policy.yaml` — same-name resource as the helm CRD, no owner declared | `done` | Root cause: the chaos approval policy only had a resource definition, without owner / managed-by metadata; `automatic-agent.io/owner` and `app.kubernetes.io/managed-by` have been added. |
| 902 | `deploy/chaos/pod-kill.yaml` — selector hits both worker and api, no `component` label isolation | `done` | Root cause: pod-kill previously only selected the whole workload via `app.kubernetes.io/name`; it is now narrowed to `app.kubernetes.io/component=api`. |
| 903 | `deploy/chaos/redis-disconnect.yaml` — lacks `duration` field, experiment defaults to infinite | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current redis disconnect manifest explicitly has `duration: "60s"`, and the target label contract has been synchronized to the README. |

## Dockerfile & docker-compose

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 904 | `Dockerfile` does not COPY `ui` and `package-lock.json`, build fails / lacks validation | `done` | Root cause: the Docker build stage did not bring UI / script-related lockfile inputs into the image context; `ui/package.json`, `ui/package-lock.json`, and related tsconfig inputs are now added. |
| 905 | `docker-compose.yml` — `POSTGRES_PASSWORD` left empty causes initial startup failure | `done` | Root cause: compose previously allowed empty-password startup; it now uses `${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?required}` for forced fail-close. |
| 906 | `Dockerfile:5` — does not copy `tsconfig.scripts.json` / `tsconfig.build-test.json`; in-image `npm run typecheck` fails | `done` | Root cause: the build stage only copied the main tsconfig; `tsconfig.scripts.json` and `tsconfig.build-test.json` are also copied into the image context. |
| 907 | `Dockerfile` — `WORKDIR /app` created by root without `chown`, under `read_only:true` the non-root user can only write to `/tmp` | `done` | Root cause: the runtime stage only `chown`-ed `/app/data` and `/tmp`; `/app` is now uniformly `chown`-ed, avoiding permission pitfalls for the non-root user under a read-only root FS. |
| 908 | `docker-compose.yml:18-21` — API container sets both `AA_PG_DSN` and the default `AA_STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`, profiles self-contradict | `done` | Root cause: the old review is still on the early `AA_PG_DSN` config; the current compose only exposes the empty-able `AA_STORAGE_POSTGRES_DSN`, no longer conflicting with the default `sqlite`. |
| 909 | `.dockerignore` — no section comments / header describing intent | `done` | Root cause: `.dockerignore` long piled up as an ungrouped blacklist; this round has added section comments grouped by git / deps / build outputs / local state. |
| 910 | `docker-compose.yml:13` — `target:runtime` references Dockerfile stage, if the stage is not declared build fails | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current Dockerfile does declare `AS runtime`. |
| 911 | `Dockerfile:1` — base image `node:22-bookworm-slim` has no digest pin | `done` | Root cause: image hardening previously only pinned the tag, not converged to a digest-level immutable reference; the base image now has a digest pin. |
| 912 | `Dockerfile` — lacks `tini` / `dumb-init` as PID 1, SIGTERM not propagated | `done` | Root cause: the runtime container directly used `node` as PID 1; `tini` is now installed and the entrypoint is `ENTRYPOINT ["tini", "--"]`. |
| 913 | `Dockerfile` — lacks OCI LABEL `org.opencontainers.image.*`, image traceability broken | `done` | Root cause: the runtime stage had no image-metadata labels; `org.opencontainers.image.*` is now added. |
| 914 | `Dockerfile` runtime stage `COPY --from=builder /app /app` — single shot copies the entire layer, breaking layered cache | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current runtime stage only copies the needed `dist/config/divisions`, not the entire directory. |
| 915 | `Dockerfile` — does not `USER node` / non-root, violating hardening | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current runtime stage explicitly `USER node`. |
| 916 | `Dockerfile` — no `HEALTHCHECK` declared | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current Dockerfile declares a `/healthz` healthcheck. |
| 917 | `Dockerfile` — `npm ci` not followed by `npm cache clean --force`, image carries npm cache | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current runtime stage runs `npm ci --omit=dev` and then `npm cache clean --force`. |
| 918 | `docker-compose.yml` — prometheus service has no healthcheck | `done` | Root cause: compose monitoring sidecar previously only had `depends_on`, no self-health probe; Prometheus now has a `/-/healthy` healthcheck. |
| 919 | `docker-compose.yml` — default tag has no sha, prod compose is not reproducible | `done` | Root cause: the boundary between local compose and production immutable-published images was not stated; the compose header now states this is the local profile, and the `AA_IMAGE_REF` env is required to pass a digest-qualified image. |
| 920 | `docker-compose.yml` — postgres / redis lack `restart: unless-stopped` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; postgres / redis now explicitly have `restart: unless-stopped`. |
| 921 | `docker-compose.yml` — does not declare `read_only` / `cap_drop ALL` | `done` | Root cause: compose hardening previously only covered some services; `cap_drop: [ALL]` is now extended to postgres / redis / prometheus / alertmanager, and services that can stay read-only do. |
| 922 | `docker-compose.yml` — postgres volume does not specify `driver_opts` / bind mount | `done` | Root cause: the postgres data volume previously only had an anonymous local volume, without a visible persistence path; it is now bound to `data/docker/postgres`. |
| 923 | `docker-compose.yml` — does not declare networks isolation | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current compose explicitly uses `automatic-agent-network`. |
| 924 | `docker-compose.yml` — `depends_on:` without `condition: service_healthy` | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current compose dependencies are orchestrated via `service_healthy`. |
| 925 | `docker-compose.override.yml` and base same-name service field merge semantics undocumented | `done` | Root cause: the review assumed the repo had an override baseline; this round has clarified in the compose header that the repo deliberately does not carry `docker-compose.override.yml`, avoiding merge-semantic drift. |
| 926 | `CONTRIBUTING.md` declares Node 20+, but Dockerfile / CI use Node 22; `engines.node` is not pinned in `package.json` | `done` | Root cause: the old CONTRIBUTING drifted from the current runtime / CI version; the current `CONTRIBUTING.md`, Dockerfile, CI, and `package.json#engines` are all unified to Node 22. |

## .github/workflows (CI)

| ID | Issue | Status | Root Cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 929 | `.github/workflows/ci.yml:4` — `workflow_call:` no `inputs` / `secrets`, callers cannot pass parameters | `done` | Root cause: the review treated regular repo CI as a reusable workflow; the current `ci.yml` is not a `workflow_call` surface, so there is no "caller cannot pass parameters" contract gap. |
| 930 | `.github/workflows/deploy-environment.yml:130-134` — on first deploy the service does not exist, blue/green slot selection falls into the default green, idempotency lost | `done` | Root cause: the first blue/green slot selection was implicitly defaulted; this round explicitly sets the first deploy to `blue` and only flips on existing selectors. |
| 931 | `.github/workflows/dr-validation.yml:60-66` — `chmod +x` depends on runtime, Windows checkout is not reproducible | `done` | Root cause: the DR workflow previously depended on runtime `chmod +x` to fix script permissions; this round has changed to directly `bash deploy/scripts/dr-drill.sh`. |
| 932 | `.github/workflows/dr-validation.yml:54-60` — DR test uses a single fake event as baseline, downstream RTO / RPO checks always pass | `done` | Root cause: the DR baseline only replayed a single placeholder event, without verifying post-recovery data shape; this round has switched to multi-event / multi-task / multi-projection seed and validates recovery counts after the drill. |
| 933 | `.github/workflows/publish-image.yml:69` — `docker login` via shell pipe injects token, should use `docker/login-action@v3` | `done` | Root cause: registry login previously used shell pipe, exposing the token in the command chain; this round has switched to `docker/login-action@v3`. |
| 934 | `.github/workflows/secret-provider-integration.yml` — only `workflow_dispatch`, no scheduled trigger | `done` | Root cause: secret-provider validation only relied on manual trigger; this round has added a weekly schedule. |
| 935 | `.github/workflows/ci.yml:148` — `node -e require(.json)` in an ESM repo only falls back to `.json`; `process.exit(1)` hard-fails on any vulnerability | `done` | Root cause: the npm audit result parsing used CJS `require` and a coarse "any vulnerability count = fail" rule; this round has switched to ESM JSON reading, failing only on high/critical vulnerabilities. |
| 936 | `.github/workflows/ci.yml:177` — `trivy exit-code:1 severity:CRITICAL,HIGH` blocks the workflow but does not upload SARIF | `done` | Root cause: image scanning only had blocking, no artifact consumable by the code-scanning system; this round outputs and uploads Trivy SARIF. |
| 937 | `.github/workflows/ci.yml:55-71` — `test:raw` / `coverage:gate` lacks `AA_RUNNING_TESTS=1`; `upload-artifact` lacks `if-no-files-found` / `compression-level` | `done` | Root cause: CI test / coverage step did not explicitly inherit the test env var, and artifact upload lacked strict mode; `AA_RUNNING_TESTS=1`, `if-no-files-found`, and compression level have been added. |
| 938 | `.github/workflows/deploy-environment.yml:159-163` — blue-green slot uses `kubectl get svc jsonpath` to read the selector, but default `selectorLabels` does not contain `instance=automatic-agent-{slot}`, SLOT is reversed | `done` | Root cause: blue/green slot derivation conflated "no selector / first deploy" with "existing blue/green selector"; the first-slot vs existing-selector judgment has been separated. |
| 939 | `.github/workflows/deploy-environment.yml:213-230` — `blue_green promote` does not delete the old slot release, dead replicas accumulate in the cluster | `done` | Root cause: the promote phase only switched the service selector, without cleaning the inactive slot release; the inactive slot is now uninstalled after promote. |
| 940 | `.github/workflows/deploy-environment.yml:262-318` — `rollback if: deploy.result=='failure'`, preflight / validate failures do not roll back even if deploy already partially executed | `done` | Root cause: the review conflated preflight failure with already-executed scenarios; preflight / validate failure happens before Helm changes, and this round continues to bind rollback to the deploy-failure path where partial changes are possible. |
| 941 | `.github/workflows/publish-image.yml:104-108` — GHA cache has no `mode=min` pruning policy, grows without bound | `done` | Root cause: the image-publish workflow previously always wrote Buildx cache as `mode=max`; this round has converged to `mode=min`. |
| 942 | `.github/workflows/ui-quality.yml:50-58` — background `vite preview` started with `&` and no trap / kill, orphan process occupies 4173 | `done` | Root cause: the UI quality gate previously started preview server in the background without lifecycle cleanup; preview PID is now recorded and a cleanup step explicitly kills it. |
| 943 | `.github/workflows/ui-quality.yml:65` — `npx playwright install` has no version pin, main version drift breaks visual snapshot | `done` | Root cause: the workflow previously used `npx playwright install` with remote resolution; this round has changed to `npm exec playwright install`, following the UI dependency version in the lockfile. |
| 944 | `.github/workflows/ui-quality.yml:25-26` — `working-directory:ui` but `upload` path uses absolute `/tmp/ui-preview.log`, beyond `GITHUB_WORKSPACE` and ignored | `done` | Root cause: the preview log used to be written to `/tmp`, and the artifact upload couldn't pick it up; it is now written to `ui/test-results/ui-preview.log`. |
| 945 | `.github/workflows/dr-validation.yml:73-75` — creates `.dr-reports/.backups/.dr-logs` in the runner workspace but not in `.gitignore`, polluting the local repo | `done` | Root cause: the DR workflow creates local directories, but `.gitignore` only ignored `.dr-reports`; `.backups/` and `.dr-logs/` have been added. |
| 946 | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — all actions only by tag, no SHA pin | `done` | Root cause: CI previously used floating tags for `actions/*`; this round has switched all to full SHA, audited by `audit-ci-supply-chain.mjs`. |
| 947 | `.github/workflows/deploy-environment.yml` — SHA pin missing; `permissions:` not minimized at job level | `done` | Root cause: workflow supply-chain governance previously only converged job-level `permissions`; third-party actions have now been brought into the full-SHA pin baseline, audited by `audit-ci-supply-chain.mjs`. |
| 948 | `.github/workflows/publish-image.yml` — `docker/login-action` tag reference + OIDC keyless not enabled | `done` | Root cause: the image-publish chain was patched in stages, with inconsistent `docker/login-action` and other third-party action pin policies; this round has unified full SHA pinning and retained the OIDC keyless signing path. |
| 949 | `.github/workflows/secret-provider-integration.yml` — lacks `concurrency` group | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current workflow has a `concurrency` group. |
| 950 | `.github/workflows/dr-validation.yml` — no `timeout-minutes`, runtime hangs forever | `done` | Root cause: the review baseline is outdated; the current DR workflow explicitly sets `timeout-minutes: 20`. |
| 951 | `.github/workflows/ui-quality.yml` — uploaded artifact has no `retention-days` | `done` | Root cause: UI artifact upload previously had no retention; `retention-days: 14` has been added. |

| 1092 | `src/sdk/cli/secret-commands.ts:116` — `left.length===right.length && timingSafeEqual` leaks length difference when files are tampered to different lengths | `done` | Root cause: the validation logic short-circuited on length inequality, breaking constant-time comparison; this round has switched to fixed-length buffer comparison, no longer exposing length difference. |
| 1093 | `src/sdk/cli/secret-commands.ts:128-129` — `mkdirSync({mode:0o700})` only applies to leaf; `writeFileSync({mode:0o600})` does not update mode if file already exists | `done` | Root cause: file permission control previously only took effect at create-time, and did not tighten existing wide permissions on overwrite; this round explicitly fixes directory and file permissions after write. |

| 1096 | `src/sdk/cli/secret-commands.ts:219` — `writeFileSync(outputPath, secretValue)` does not check if target is a symlink, TOCTOU can write to arbitrary paths | `done` | Root cause: secret materialization previously used a normal write-file path without a no-follow constraint; this round uses a safe file-write path and `O_NOFOLLOW` to prevent symlink traversal. |

| 1101 | `src/sdk/cli/inspect.ts` — `JSON.stringify(snapshot)` has no truncation / streaming for large snapshots, exceeding stdout high-water mark loses fields | `done` | Root cause: the inspect output previously did a one-shot `JSON.stringify` and wrote the whole block to stdout, without considering oversized snapshots and backpressure; this round has added truncated serialization and chunked output. |

| 1446 | `web/vite.config.ts:56` — SRI regex does not recognize existing `integrity=`, double injection | `done` | Root cause: the SRI injection logic once didn't recognize existing `integrity`; tags with existing `integrity` are now explicitly skipped. |

| 1449 | `workflow-cockpit/web/dag-viewer.tsx:107` — `position:absolute` + `zIndex:-1`, parent container not `relative`, connection lines visually escape the panel | `done` | Root cause: DAG connection lines previously relied on negative z-index absolute positioning; they are now a normal-flow rail layout, no longer escaping the container. |
| 1450 | `workflow-cockpit/web/dag-viewer.tsx:38-46` — `branchGroups` without `useMemo`, O(n) recompute on large workflow | `done` | Root cause: DAG branch groups were recomputed on every render; `branchGroups` and `stage steps` are now memoized. |

| 1454 | `workflow-builder/web/index.tsx:21` — fixed `height:280` + `overflow:hidden`, `MiniMap` / `Controls` overlap on narrow screens | `done` | Root cause: workflow builder canvas container once hardcoded 280 height and clipped overflow; it is now responsive height and visible overflow layout. |
